# SHINE POST V2 — Evidence and Claim Ledger

## Responsibility and present state

The independent ledger runs through E12. Dated appendices give the changes supported by the direct E01–E12 comparison, including the cases reserved for fresh source inspection. [Comparison and adjudication audit](../07%20Audits%20and%20Handoffs/SHINEPOST_V2_CROSS_MODEL_RECONCILIATION_AUDIT.md)

This ledger is the canonical cumulative home for observation routes, claims, competing interpretations, uncertainty and V1/candidate→V2 revisions under the [method](../00%20Frameworks/SHINEPOST_V2_ANALYTICAL_METHOD.md) and [architecture](../00%20Frameworks/SHINEPOST_V2_SYNTHESIS_ARCHITECTURE.md). The independent episode sections own observation IDs, claims and unresolved questions. Administrative bootstrap inventory belongs in the source register and manifest.

Preserve source fact, inference, interpretation, speculative hypothesis and value judgment as distinct statement kinds. `TXT/FRM/CAV/AO/HL/AM` identify evidence routes; `INF/INT` identify analytical reasoning. Confidence, artifact authority and claim transition are separate dimensions. A high-confidence report is not a primary source.

## Correction to the E01 closing lyric

**SPV2-REV-E01-01 · REVISE the gloss in SPV2-PF-E01-05.** The original performance record describes “wanting to lie.” In the E01 Japanese subtitle witness, cue 397 (1260.910–1265.370 s) denies that there is any lie the lyric speaker wants to tell. The negative existential `あるわけないよ` scopes over `つきたい嘘`, a lie one wants to tell. Cue 400 (1265.370–1270.830 s) gives the speaker's resolve to keep believing and turn something into truth. The two lyric cues share a boundary. The intervening numbered cues 398 and 399 are spoken dialogue in a separate subtitle style.

**PRESERVE SPV2-CLM-E01-14.** The frozen E01 reading's “wish not to tell a lie” is a compatible loose paraphrase. The narrower grammatical claim is the denial of a desire to tell a lie. The qualified claim that the closing lyric can illuminate the cousins' conversation survives the correction. The caption gives neither cousin a private wish to deceive. Its connection to their conversation remains an interpretation of the edit. This revision changes the lyric gloss in the performance record. It supplies no new character motive or directed relationship. The words as sung, vocal allocation and musical synchronization remain open.

The evidence is TXT from the admitted SPV2-W-E01-JP witness in SPV2-W-E01-ZIP. The selected ASS SHA-256 is `ce93a24660eea14d9a4b31b10e33652dbf2d14520ceedf41e48ea45bd7f80b00`. The dialogue-index SHA-256 is `1a7c3f275f34a1de455644bca0c208432d8e605c2835d895e7983e3e52579094`. The two rows have style `ED-JP_EP01`. The responsible musical event remains in the [performance ledger](SHINEPOST_V2_PERFORMANCE_AND_FORM_LEDGER.md#correction-to-the-e01-closing-lyric).

## Stable records

Assign IDs only to real records. IDs remain stable when formulations change; revisions have their own IDs.

| Prefix / record | Required fields |
| --- | --- |
| `SPV2-OBS-…` observation | Exact witness/derivative, episode and bounded locator, inspected channel/route, observer, exposure, observation, evidence class, uncertainty, resolution/reinspection and reported/anchored/disputed status where applicable |
| `SPV2-CLM-…` claim | Current wording, statement kind, source boundary, supporting observations, reasoning, strongest rival, counterevidence, confidence/rationale, disconfirmation condition, topical home and dependent outputs |
| `SPV2-REV-…` revision | Claim ID, earlier formulation and exact original route, transition, proposed/current formulation, new evidence, alternative readings, reviewer objection/disposition, authority scope and propagation results |
| `SPV2-OPEN-…` unresolved question | Precise question, affected claims, missing evidence/channel, materiality, competing answers, next resolving operation and any blocker |

A locator combines stable witness, episode, calibrated source interval and cue/frame/content anchor as appropriate. Source text keeps Japanese wording, speaker/addressee, witness/provenance and translation notes separate. Quote only the amount analytically needed. Unverified listening or derivative transcription is not silently promoted to TXT. Contact-sheet positions alone are navigation, not exact visual citations.

## Chronology, disclosure and constraints

Record recurring story chronology, audience disclosures and institutional/world constraints here when they shape more than one local reading. Preserve presentation boundary versus inferred story time, who knows each fact, confidence and counterexamples. Individual knowledge state links to the character ledger; it does not duplicate a second incompatible chronology.

A proposed world/institution rule is a claim with scope and exceptions, not a universal fact by default. Model familiarity, genre patterns, another adaptation or V1 prose can suggest a question, but cannot supply fresh V2 source evidence. Bibliographic and administrative claims identify their own source category and carry no narrative evidentiary weight.

## Claim transitions and intake comparison

| Transition | Use |
| --- | --- |
| `PRESERVE` | Reviewed earlier formulation remains supported at its stated scope |
| `STRENGTHEN` | Additional evidence supports a specified stronger or more secure formulation |
| `REVISE` | Evidence supports a materially different formulation |
| `DOWNGRADE` | Narrow the claim, confidence or scope to what the evidence supports |
| `REJECT` | Identified evidence defeats the claim at the reviewed scope |
| `OPEN` | Material uncertainty or disagreement remains unresolved |

No transition has been assigned merely because V1 exists or Antigravity is reported complete. On review, retain the exact original artifact identity and claim passage, not a weakened paraphrase. Record preserved findings as well as disagreements. No V1-wide supersession follows from accepting one corrected claim or a new method.

For candidate integration, compare original/base, returned candidate and current target. Resolve mechanical drift separately from literary disagreement. A substantive correction requires evidence, counterevidence and a recoverable reasoning chain. If authorized correction is blocked, record the affected claim, evidence, exact obstacle and narrow remedy; do not leave the uncertainty hidden in a peripheral note.

## Observation disputes and historical integrity

Use the [AV protocol](../00%20Frameworks/SHINEPOST_V2_AV_RECONCILIATION_AND_INTAKE_PROTOCOL.md) for capability-limited disputes: one specific return, then at most one independent verification route where warranted. Continued disagreement stays OPEN. Do not confuse inability to inspect with refutation, or agreement between models with source certainty.

Frozen candidates, source receipts, independent logs and prospective predictions preserve their original bytes. Current claim views may change with append-only revisions linking those originals. A post-exposure observation must be marked as such. Do not reconstruct a supposedly prospective freeze after reading the ending or the candidate's interpretation.

## Propagation and acceptance

Only the accountable integrator writes shared state. Each material accepted revision records affected character, relationship and performance IDs; episode or synthesis sections; and any model/validation dependencies. Update them together or leave the dependent completion visibly blocked. Record actual reviewers and inspected evidence, not fictional role labels.

Before semantic acceptance, perform the handoff contract's bounded name/reference review of the synopsis/introduction, a substantive middle section and the conclusion/exit state, extending to consequential relationships. Record sampled scope, ambiguous references, corrections and remaining limits. This tests whether a reader can follow who says, believes or does what; it is not a name-frequency exercise.

The bootstrap's empty analytical record state is intentional. Populate it through actual admitted work and preserve the distinction between a usable schema and an evidentially supported conclusion.

## E01 · independent records

Episode and presentation cutoff E01; the 2018 Budokan event and Naoki’s employment memory belong to earlier story time. Witness keys are `SPV2-W-E01-SRC` (release), `-JP` (Japanese-only fan ASS), `-ZIP` (derived frame/indices) and `-AO` (main FLAC). Numeric locators are release playback seconds. A cue means a one-based row of the Japanese dialogue index; f denotes the numeric manifest prefix. The same Japanese captions burned into the JPGs repeat the transcription witness. SRC indicates direct original-picture samples. All source readers named in rows were confined to E01 during independent reading. The integrator reconciled independently frozen contributions after critical review. Anchored observations retain the limitations stated in each row. The episode checkpoint binds the contributions by hash. No V1 or candidate comparison was undertaken.

### Revisions and retained questions at the E03 cutoff

- STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E02-05 and distinguish the questions recorded together in SPV2-OPEN-E02-04. The trio hears the dedicated manager describe his new standing before the next sale. Fans queue and all tickets sell for one more show. SPV2-OBS-E03-13 STRENGTHENS the reception question in SPV2-CLM-E02-44 and SPV2-CLM-E02-45. The green-clad evaluative spectator and Miku can be traced across shows. The durability of the audience and eventual Nakano result remain unknown.
- PRESERVE the visual and acoustic limits on the E02 dance diagnosis. SPV2-OBS-E03-05 adds Momiji's retrospectively attributed account of lateness. Her rivalry, the exact step and performers' intentions are separate evidential matters.
- SPV2-OPEN-E03-01, HIGH. Who actually sings, falls silent or covers at each instant in the historical performance and in the dream? Match synchronized original main audio and picture, preserving the difference between incident and subjective restaging. Downstream historical ability, Haru's choice, and PF center events.
- SPV2-OPEN-E03-02, HIGH. What does Kyoka privately want, and how does she understand Haru's intervention, Naoki's decision and Tokka's request? Her refusals, fan recall and the manager's inference disagree in scope. Downstream CH-KYOKA and directed relationships.
- SPV2-OPEN-E03-03, MODERATE. How much does Tokka know about the original incident and from whose point of view? Firsthand gaze and her words establish particular interest, not knowledge of the other performers' interior experience.
- SPV2-OPEN-E03-04, HIGH. Does Kyoka accept or carry out the announced center, does Rio handle the MC work, and what does the audience experience? The source ends at an assignment. Preserve forecast separately from later outcome.
- SPV2-OPEN-E03-05, MODERATE. Exact delivery, deliberate pauses, comedically layered voices and sound/body synchrony in the Spiral, hypnosis and disclosure are open pending competent audio or continuous-AV inspection.

### E03 visual observations and claim contributions

Clock is elapsed release seconds. IDs in the source column without a prefix refer to the E03 retained-frame manifest. FRM includes original decode stills and consecutive native pictures, never a claim of continuous audiovisual perception. TXT is the selected Japanese subtitle witness; the 1,209 cue rows carry no reliable speaker assignment. Inferences below use only material admitted through E03.

#### Visual claims

| Record | Class, confidence, observations | Formulation and revision boundary |
|---|---|---|
| SPV2-CLM-E03-51 | INT high; OBS 51, 60, 61 | Repeated stage geometry lets the dream remove the practical help the narrated event restores. In the feared version spectators seem remote and flanks shadowed. In the recollection Rio continues, Haru crosses and takes the foreground, and Kyoka resumes choreography. Historical refusal by either partner, or real collective disapproval, cannot be inferred from the dream. |
| SPV2-CLM-E03-52 | INT high; OBS 52–55, 62 | Success becomes an obstacle to noticing an individual hesitation. A crowd waiting before recruitment, hands emptying stacks, and Rio’s celebration register E02's changed conditions; only Tokka's prolonged exchange, Haru's glance, held profile at the window and Rio's interruption carry Kyoka's private change forward. Time, headcount estimate and individual transactions remain distinct. |
| SPV2-CLM-E03-53 | INT high; OBS 55–58 | Girls recognize behavioral changes through deviations from familiar exchanges. Rio uses contact, Yukine misses Kyoka’s usually prompt joke, Haru improvises hypnosis and Momiji unwittingly publicizes the plan. Kyoka’s ability to interrupt and physically hold the conspirators qualifies an account of pure helplessness. Their good intentions do not establish her assent. |
| SPV2-CLM-E03-54 | INT high for sites; interpretation of the first statement’s exact clause OPEN; OBS 57, 59, 64 | Distinguish visually marked denials at practice and in the group conversation, the white-framed recollection of a more complex statement, and the ordinary coloring during the later private singing denial. A lack of shown gold at the latter cannot decide whether she desires the song or consents to the assignment. |
| SPV2-CLM-E03-55 | FRM + TXT + INT high; OBS 60–61 | In the narrated past performance the rescuing action literally puts Kyoka out of view momentarily. Her continued dancing and Tokka's concentrated attention coexist with the missed opening. The lyric lapse and Haru's vocal cover are reported; exact audio onset, first recovered syllable, later Kyoka vocal contribution and complete choreography-to-beat relationship stay OPEN. |
| SPV2-CLM-E03-56 | INT high; OBS 51, 54, 61–64 | Repeated costume, braid and face connect an initially anonymous spectator, current ticket buyer and participant at the handshake. Tokka’s separate recollection starts with Kyoka’s street-level solicitation and returns to a request for this particular song. That continuing attention supplies an answer to fear of losing one fan; it does not establish what every witness thought during the first performance. |
| SPV2-CLM-E03-57 | INT high; OBS 58, 59, 63, 64 | Contact can both offer access and end it. The pinning of two shoulders gives Kyoka comic control; accepting Tokka’s hand makes room for an unusually personal exchange; Naoki's extended arm enforces its time limit. His stated reason is procedural. A private plan to engineer the fan’s final words is unconfirmed. |
| SPV2-CLM-E03-58 | INT high; OBS 55, 59, 64–66 | Removing the role Kyoka invokes to justify remaining at the side prompts her to stand. The rehearsal room already places her alone opposite Naoki when he names the closer. Announcement, practical willingness, and eventual stage result are distinct; the final face and ED leave the latter two unresolved. |

Original-picture cross-check: 164.581 s confirms the title over Haru; 656.114, 819.027 and 833.875 distinguish two present denials from the remembered E01 statement. The original at 1037.828–1052.426 identifies illustrated paper offered on the street, preceding the later named request at the handshake. The means of attendance at that first concert remains uncertain.

## E04 · independent evidence and claim history

Registered witness family SPV2-W-E04-SRC, -ZIP, -JP and -AO. Times are release seconds. Cue numbers are one-based positions in the full 1,133-row index. The 780 opening graphic entries precede the first lexical row, C781. FRM includes indexed samples and bounded native probes. The JP script and derivative overlaid subtitles share ancestry. AM is the verified Japanese program mix. E04 is the latest narrative available.

### Revision history and retained questions

- STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E03-04 and E03-05 with a first-person account of the earlier choice and a recoverable promise to Tokka. Keep both E03's prospective account and the now-attested E04 disclosures.
- REVISE the scope of SPV2-OPEN-E03-02. Kyoka now tells Naoki that she wants to remain an idol and stand on a bigger stage. Her own earlier request for center is shown. The original mental lapse, her comparative feelings toward Haru and the pressure of the new assignment still have several possible explanations. See SPV2-OBS-E04-04, SPV2-OBS-E04-05, SPV2-OBS-E04-07 and SPV2-CLM-E04-03.
- STRENGTHEN the answer to SPV2-OPEN-E03-03 with Kyoka's firsthand promise to Tokka and the fan's presence at both performances. Tokka's knowledge of what Kyoka was thinking when the first entrance failed stays OPEN. See SPV2-OBS-E04-05 and SPV2-OBS-E04-58.
- REVISE SPV2-OPEN-E03-04 at the E04 cutoff. Rio performs the MC handoff and Kyoka takes center for the closer. A returning fan reacts visibly. The sound of the individual entrances, the rest of the audience's private experience and longer-term attendance remain OPEN. See SPV2-OBS-E04-09, SPV2-OBS-E04-10, SPV2-OBS-E04-11 and SPV2-PF-E04-08.
- PRESERVE SPV2-OPEN-E03-01 for exact sound in the failed opener. E04 gives an internal account of mental blankness, and explanatory uncertainty remains in the very word she uses for its cause.
- SPV2-OPEN-E04-01, HIGH. Which singer handles the entry and later lines at the present concert, and what audible junctures distinguish it from the earlier failed entrance? Compare synchronized program audio for the historical and present versions. Affects both song events and the interpretation of Haru's cover.
- SPV2-OPEN-E04-02, MODERATE. How are Hotaru's invitation, backing, room response, imagined recognition and restaurant conversation voiced and mixed? Text and wide-window energy cannot establish the sound of intimacy, silence or humor.
- SPV2-OPEN-E04-03, MODERATE. How much did Yukine know, and how did she come to see Kyoka? The location is accurate, the picture can place her professed coincidence in doubt, and the alternative remains unspecified.
- SPV2-OPEN-E04-04, MODERATE. What happens after Tokka lowers out of view, and does Kyoka know how personally this gesture was received? Ending frame withholds any exchange or explanation.
- SPV2-OPEN-E04-05, HIGH. Can this particular appearance change group recognition or future attendance? One sold-out regular show and a single fan's response cannot settle the larger Nakano objective.

## E05 · Training, television and the work of recruiting an audience

Witness family SPV2-W-E05-SRC / ZIP / JP / AO. Times are zero-origin release seconds. Numbered C routes are one-based full dialogue-index rows. The source-authorized independent basis is TXT, FRM and instrumented AM. JP is a fan ASS family whose render and machine export share ancestry. INF and INT indicate how the observations are joined.

### Dated revisions from the E05 cutoff

- SPV2-REV-E05-01 · STRENGTHEN the discouragement reading in SPV2-CLM-E02-34. PRESERVE the uncertainty about offscreen conduct in SPV2-CLM-E02-24. Two matched pictures make the earlier sale the specific object of Naoki's recollection, and E05 explicitly shows a link between Rio's difficulty selling, her walkout and her continued recruitment work today. No recovered image shows where she spent the entire E02 break. This later distinction affects SPV2-CH-RIO, SPV2-REL-NAOKI-RIO and the interpretation of her changing audience work. See SPV2-OBS-E05-10, SPV2-OBS-E05-13 and SPV2-CLM-E05-09.
- SPV2-REV-E05-02 · STRENGTHEN evidence about the audience for Kyoka's center performance following SPV2-PF-E04-08 and SPV2-CLM-E04-06. Naoki says that an attendee became Kyoka's fan at the last live and offered his shop. The shop event in turn brings particular visitors to the ticket sale. This is one identified consequence beyond the returning supporters known at E04. The sales result for the whole venue and Kyoka's wider celebrity remain OPEN. Affects SPV2-CH-KYOKA, her outward fan relationships and the two public-facing E05 jobs. See SPV2-OBS-E05-04, SPV2-OBS-E05-09 and SPV2-CLM-E05-04.

### Japanese-language evidence

Release-relative times. TXT is the selected Japanese fan ASS as numbered in bundle/dialogue_index.json. FRM numbers are program images in the bundle root. Original refers to a directly decoded native frame of muxed_jp_softsub/shinepost_ep05.jp-softsub.mkv. The selected ASS has empty speaker fields. Inferences about speakers and the significance of their wording are identified separately.

### Images and embodied performance

Source addresses below use E05 release presentation seconds, the retained-picture manifest index when given, and the Japanese dialogue index cue number when a line matters. FRM denotes sampled images, including native consecutive decoded images at specifically identified windows. TXT denotes written material. The numbered observations identify what another reader can check. The claims include their dependencies and a competing interpretation or limit.

Her arm goes in, the hand returns empty, the door closes, and she turns before her school-uniformed partners greet her. She changes to a hand-on-hip, chin-up pose. |

#### Visual claims

| ID | Evidence, source route and limits |
| --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-51 | OBS 51, 52, 65. Rio has grounds for feeling comparatively overlooked while she also has people who value her. Her scrolling and Miku's visit supply the two sides. Three visible comments and individual customers cannot estimate popularity. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-52 | OBS 53, 54. The leading foot and sweeping hair make the turn itself pleasurable to watch. The second shoe, wobble and vacant whiteboard stage the real joke as a failure to finish. Her named difference of rotation comes from cue 809. Exact count, pace against sound, and contact with the floor are unresolved. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-53 | OBS 52, 55, 61. Repeated local brightening asks us to reconsider prepared assurances from several speakers. Rio's isolated mouth makes her outward choice readable before its motive is clear. The effect alone cannot designate the precise concealed thought. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-54 | OBS 56, 57, 58, 65. The shop gives the girls a bag to pass directly across a counter. On location Rio must address an apparatus while a crew watches. Both jobs have live witnesses, and the counter's later images give the shop an observable consequence. Individual shopper-to-ticket conversions remain only partly specified. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-55 | OBS 58–60. Professionally usable material can come from Rio's loss of composure. The director's thumb and dialogue support that reading of the harvest. The same images also work as slapstick for the episode's audience. Whether any of them appeared in a final broadcast remains open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-56 | OBS 60–63. The pepper reverses the favorable response to accidental comedy. In a close view, a fraction of a second gives an open eye enormous weight. A wide view counts the witnesses to a public failure, then the director and Naoki accept different responsibilities. Satō speaks about the tasting segment. Neither a completed broadcast nor rejection of the whole shoot follows. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-57 | OBS 64–68. Remaining apart can be an active decision to carry some of the group's work. The papers leaving the counter, their destination and the prior-looking insert substantiate Naoki's new understanding. The inset makes a comparison and does not independently establish every reason for an earlier departure. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-58 | OBS 65, 67, 69. The booth has begun selling Nakano tickets, while what changes hands at sunset is a promotional sheet for the group's own theater. It gives Rio a lower threshold for an initial encounter, a request for attention which can precede a request to attend. The date and print alone do not establish when the street encounter took place. |
| SPV2-CLM-E05-59 | OBS 67–70. The episode gives the stranger, Momiji and Naoki different actions in response to Rio. Only the stranger takes the leaflet. The manager understands something about Rio and the companion watches. Neither can supply the stranger's answer about attending a future performance. The title sequence does not close that question. |

## E06 · A new song, an old obligation and a test in public

Source objects SPV2-W-E06-SRC, SPV2-W-E06-ZIP, SPV2-W-E06-JP and SPV2-W-E06-AO. C numbers identify one-based rows of the dialogue index. Times use zero-origin local release seconds. TXT includes production lettering and the separately identified fallible Japanese subtitle. FRM includes sampled original-release pictures, AM describes the main stereo mix. Each source has distinct limits on what the reader can recover.

### Principal observations

| Stable ID | Evidence and locator | What the source establishes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-01 | FRM/TXT 0–49.5 and 141.41–201.53, C1–20/801–832; original 25.108, 47.506, 49.466, 156.406, 199.533 | Kyoka volunteers first, Haru claims her turn, Rio asserts it can only be herself, with a visible gold outline. Naoki had already decided. He assigns Rio, removes almost all dance for this song; her public assertion gives way to startled denial. Haru and Kyoka accept the form. Kyoka's opposition to refusing center prompts Haru's aside. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-02 | TXT/FRM 202.13–291.65, C833–869; originals 220.429, 247.914, 251.835, 255.505, 264.723, 274.524 | Rio leaves; Momiji pursues, holds her arm, proposes teaching dance and asks her to return. Rio insists she will improve by herself. Haru and Kyoka arrive. Rio gets away and pauses near a Hotaru advert. She explicitly wants to help other people and make them smile. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-03 | FRM/TXT 293.79–381.81, C870–899; originals 304.012, 315.232, 347.305, 353.228, 359.526, 376.626 | Aoi proposes borrowing the forgotten book from another class; a book is offered to Rio. Rio wins a ticket drawing for Hotaru's concert for Aoi's sake. Despite the thanks and delight, she credits Hotaru with Aoi's smile. The board identifies the SHINING TOUR Final 2018 in Budokan. Crowds make Rio feel as if she might disappear; she asks what the live offers beyond recordings and videos. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-04 | TXT/FRM/AM 382–437.30, C900–913 and end card 1264.805; orig 385.302, 392.017, 397.022, 402.235, 408.033, 414.039, 418.001, 431.431 | Hotaru, the backing dancers, the individual spectators and the stage screen are shown during Rio's recollection. Rio describes the singing, dance and her increasing energy, then perceives an effect on others. The closing credits list Sweet Surrender, sung by Hotaru. Principal mix windows 385–403.46/403.47–416.31/416.77–437.30 measure −21.84/−24.33/−25.07 dBFS RMS. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-05 | FRM/TXT 440–582.14, C914–956; orig 444.110, 450.033, 456.915, 466.007, 473.014, 484.025, 488.113, 497.622 | Phone applications and rejection notices precede the Brightest audition. Rio falls in front of three judges, Yuki speaks of the kindness of her shine and the later result, and Rio celebrates at home. Recollections then show her onstage error, support from Kyoka and Haru and her decision to keep up a confident face lest she worry others. Acceptance, competence and persona come at different points. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-06 | TXT/FRM 586.36–702.04, C957–985; orig 603.520, 610.735, 612.904, 647.814, 668.835, 672.213, 695.403 | Night swing, recollection of Momiji's grip, remorse and a 20:05 lock screen. Rio returns for secret practice. Naoki is at the practice room. She objects to the way he has staged his entrance, then says she has come for something forgotten. The independently shown intended exercise discredits that account for the viewer. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-07 | TXT/FRM 702.04–877.44, C986–1044; orig 712.503/714.005/718.009/725.808/729.312/744.535/804.804/855.021/871.704 | In the current room the forgotten-object excuse (698.031, 699.866) and the later self-sufficiency boast (710.001, 713.713) each have gold. Ordinary light returns at 718.009. The gold-marked wish for praise at 724.474 is a recollection. Naoki names kindness behind her perceived arrogance. She identifies being a burden, continuously receiving help and falling short of Hotaru; he acknowledges comparative weaknesses. He asks her to learn the strength from the song in performance, then urges her to take center. Rio agrees and widens the beneficiaries of her promise from two named partners to everyone. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-08 | FRM/TXT 888–930.93, C1045–1054; orig 899.941, 905.113, 907.406, 914.831, 919.752, 929.929 | Rio presents Momiji with a 甘天堂 pudding; both sides of the transfer are shown. She asks for instruction in dance if she succeeds at the live. Momiji agrees. Yukine peeks around the edge of the doorway. Nobody here has yet given Rio a dance lesson. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-09 | FRM/TXT 932.431–1040.27, C1055–1087; orig 940.106, 941.941, 967.425, 975.766, 1015.014, 1032.031 | Mall in the Ikebukuro setting. | SPV2-OBS-E06-10 | TXT/FRM/AM 1046.90–1200.27, C1088–1116; orig 1065.731, 1076.534, 1092.008, 1118.034, 1125.041, 1148.022, 1174.423, 1195.820 | Her partners introduce the new appeal. | SPV2-OBS-E06-11 | TXT/FRM/AM 1200.27–1289.50, C1117–1124; orig 1201.200, 1204.328, 1214.004, 1227.309, 1241.031, 1254.837, 1261.135, 1264.805, 1272.021, 1283.032 | Credit illustrations run through the later lyrics, including school, practice, farm and street promotion. Ending card identifies the trio TiNgS for Yellow Rose; a different card credits Hotaru's Sweet Surrender. The final lyric about a bouquet ends 1275.00; a picture with flyers and credits persists afterward. Across 1200.27–1275 programme RMS is −19.95 dBFS; across 1275–1289.50 it is −38.86 with 54.41% of 10 ms bins below −45 dBFS. |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-12 | TXT/FRM/AM 1289.50–end, C1125–1147; orig 1294.001, 1304.803, 1324.031, 1360.609, 1363.571, 1364.613, 1369.910 | Rio tentatively addresses the floor. The audience raises hands/lights, praises the singing and chants her name; some ask what group she belongs to. Sato approves. Rio's regal phrase breaks off; she cries, says she did it and receives both girls' encouragement. Her renewed Nakano boast overlaps Naoki turning to a next task. View past him to the opposite rail, then Momiji with Yukine. The last original sample at 1369.910 is black. |

### Principal claims

| Stable ID | Interpretation and support | Limits, rival or test and downstream record |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-01 | INF high, from SPV2-OBS-E06-01/10. The three girls' wish for the same professional opportunity can coexist with the two unselected girls' active support for the person chosen. | Competitive teasing alone is possible at the opening. Kyoka's voluntary bid is consequential after E04. Look for independent repeated preference. Affects Kyoka, Haru and their support for Rio. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-02 | INT high for the association Rio makes. She takes reduction of dance as a cost her incapacity has imposed on everyone, which explains why removing her chief difficulty feels like grounds *against* centering. SPV2-OBS-E06-01/02/07. | Fidelity to TiNgS's customary sound and dance is also an express reason and recurs among spectators. There is no general vote against changing the act. Rio→Haru/Kyoka, Naoki→Rio. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-03 | INT high. Rio's ability to arrange an experience for someone is already on display when Aoi thanks her; Rio discounts that contribution in favor of Hotaru, anticipating how she credits her groupmates. SPV2-OBS-E06-03/04 and SPV2-OBS-E05-11. | Aoi's admiration is real and the light show independently affects Rio. The two sources of pleasure can operate together. Aoi→Rio, Rio→Hotaru and her later public role. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-04 | INT high for Rio's narrated trajectory. Hotaru answers a specific fear of disappearing in a crowd by helping Rio recognize something first in herself and then in those around her. SPV2-OBS-E06-03/04. | Rio can observe faces and responses, not everyone's inner life. E01 and E04 had distinct interests at this same named engagement. Preserve each episode's horizon. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-05 | INF high for the projected motive in her own account. The royal persona acquires a history as a deliberate way to spare her partners worry after she repeatedly experienced herself as less capable. SPV2-OBS-E06-05/07/12. | Genuine competitive play, frustration, affection and the satisfaction of being singled out survive this account. Her final reuse of the name is counterevidence to any claim that growth requires discarding it. State of Rio and her relationships in the trio. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-06 | INF high. Naoki recognizes the local falsity of the self-sufficiency boast and reconstructs a motive from observed history. Rio's account of her childhood is independently offered to the viewer. SPV2-OBS-E06-03/05/06/07. | The brilliance cue does not reveal what was said to Aoi or what took place at the audition. Opening a future conversation may add to his knowledge; it cannot be silently backdated. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-07 | INT high. Naoki challenges the move from weaknesses to worthlessness and designs an audience test rather than rating each skill aloud. Rio enters the test only after privately consenting. SPV2-OBS-E06-01/07/12. | Both the selected role and its terms originate with him. She was genuinely unwilling in the afternoon. Persuasion and pressure are compatible; long-term freedom of choice is untested. Naoki→Rio, Rio→audience. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-08 | INF high. The pudding and conditional, polite request transform an existing offer of help into something Rio actively solicits. SPV2-OBS-E06-02/08 and SPV2-OBS-E05-03. | Terms leave some of the old rule intact: she first aims to earn help. No evidence here establishes the lesson occurred, who bought the pudding or that Yukine heard the full arrangement. Momiji↔Rio. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-09 | INT high. Introducing a center familiar fans did not anticipate tests a particular route to a wider audience, the immediate appeal of singing, and also changes what existing fans know to value. SPV2-OBS-E06-09/10/12 and SPV2-CLM-E05-02. | Some people were fans of Rio before she sang this number. Sato was expressly invited, and one stranger asking a group name establishes interest without a sale. No general proof of future demand. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-10 | INT high. Choosing a song about loneliness within performed invulnerability makes shared protection an intelligible public offer while allowing the television viewer privileged access to what it cost. SPV2-OBS-E06-07/10/11. | Production credits acknowledge three singers. The song's first person need not be a literal autobiography, or the mall audience's interpretation identical to the viewer's. Musical/formal and interpersonal consequences. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-11 | INF high for represented change in Rio's knowledge. Her broken-off boast and declaration to her friends that she managed roots the success in her own experience. SPV2-OBS-E06-07/12. | It took others' audible-in-world and depicted responses first. Self-recognition does not certify durable confidence, general technical proficiency, commercial results or resolution of her complex about dance. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-12 | INF moderate. Naoki's unfinished thought about the next challenge leads into a look toward Momiji and Yukine. SPV2-OBS-E06-12. | The editorial association does not supply the contemplated proposal, a future group arrangement or either girl's wishes. Other people share the railing. Preserve as an open direction rather than a decision. |

### Questions left open at this boundary

- SPV2-OPEN-E06-01 · Individual voices/lines, timbre, instrumentation, exact starts and transitions, separation of speech and performance, and sound-to-motion alignment in the two consequential performances. Candidate reinspection needs main-program AO/CAV with calibrated clocks and credit/lyric comparison.
- SPV2-OPEN-E06-02 · Whether Rio begins the agreed dance lessons, whether she can ask spontaneously another time, and how long her new self-assessment survives; a later observed lesson or explicit response is required.
- SPV2-OPEN-E06-03 · Effects on Nakano tickets, total attendance, subsequent invitations and the eventual status of any previously approached pedestrian. A dated booking, published result or identifying later encounter would change the evidence.
- SPV2-OPEN-E06-04 · The origin/date of each illustrated flyer encounter in the ending montage. The images extend evidence of Rio's street labor, while her whereabouts throughout the E02 absence and the identity of each passerby remain qualified.
- SPV2-OPEN-E06-05 · What Naoki intends after watching the opposite balcony, and what Yukine and Momiji want independently of his intentions. The final look establishes the juxtaposition only.

### Comparison with an earlier spectator

| Stable ID | Source comparison and limitation |
| --- | --- |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-13 | FRM/TXT. E06 f000457 at 336.000 has a passing girl in a ruffled white collar, red ribbon and dark ponytail with a pink tie in front of Rio, Aoi and their taller companion. E04 f000404 at 295.837 and f000411 at 306.000 show the corresponding profile, hair ornaments and clothes on Kyoka. E06 original 376.626 names the Budokan 2018 tour finale. The two groups exchange no visible greeting in this cut. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-13 | INF high for an identifiable visual crossing. The E04 clothing, profile and hair detail support Kyoka's presence in the immediate foreground of Rio's recollection. The audience can recognize her while the girls' own knowledge of each other remains unestablished. Preserves SPV2-CLM-E04-05 and adds a shared physical context to SPV2-CLM-E06-04. An affirmative earlier meeting would need its own evidence. |

### The possible returning passerby

| Stable ID | Source, interpretation and limit |
| --- | --- |
| SPV2-OBS-E06-14 | FRM. E06 f001274 at 1305.617 shows a long-haired woman holding a yellow lightstick, eyes closed and smiling, with a short-haired spectator in blue beside her. E05 f001269 at 1248.000 and f001294 at 1272.730 show the woman who takes a flyer. Side-part, forehead lock, ear and proportions correspond. Clothing and companions do not supply a second unique identification. E05's sheet names the Bright Station regular live. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-14 | INF moderate. A visible resemblance raises the possibility that the E05 passerby is now in Rio's audience. Common character design remains a serious alternative. A generic “passerby” cast credit cannot assign a specific visible person, and attendance at this mall would not establish redemption of a handbill for a different venue. This qualifies the open audience question attached to SPV2-CLM-E05-10 and SPV2-OPEN-E06-03; it does not close that question. |

### Revisions earned at the E06 cutoff

- SPV2-REV-E06-01 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E05-07 and SPV2-CLM-E05-10. Rio's narrated decision after her early stage error identifies a chosen purpose of the superior persona: protect her colleagues from worry while she feels unable to help. Her rejection of Momiji and later request for assistance disclose a cost to both people. This adds firsthand represented testimony to E05's evidence from public action. It leaves the precise mix of motives at the earlier sweetshop decision qualified. Affects SPV2-CH-RIO, SPV2-REL-NAOKI-RIO, SPV2-REL-MOMIJI-RIO and Rio's relations to both partners. See SPV2-OBS-E06-05, SPV2-OBS-E06-07, SPV2-OBS-E06-08.
- SPV2-REV-E06-02 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E05-02. The mall provides an actual test of a skill that visitors with little knowledge of the group can recognize. People drawn to the rail say how well Rio sings and ask about the group; Sato's assessment is especially informative alongside her earlier professional judgment and her statement that she rarely goes to idol shows. This is one documented outcome of one number. Preserve the open commercial effects and earlier fan attention to Rio. Affects SPV2-CH-RIO, SPV2-CH-NAOKI, the mall-live event and their respective relationships to the public. See SPV2-OBS-E06-09 through SPV2-OBS-E06-12.
- SPV2-REV-E06-03 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E04-03. Kyoka now claims a new center assignment on her own initiative and remains willing to support another selected performer. This extends the evidence about her appetite for the role beyond E04's promise to a particular fan. It does not convert E04's initially unilateral assignment into a freely chosen one. Affects SPV2-CH-KYOKA and her directed relations to Naoki and Rio. See SPV2-OBS-E06-01, SPV2-OBS-E06-10.
- SPV2-REV-E06-04 · PRESERVE the chronological caution in SPV2-CLM-E05-09 and SPV2-REV-E05-01. The ending drawings give further visual evidence of Rio with flyers in different settings, including a comparatively dark street by a bookseller and a sunlit offer. They provide no contemporaneous date identifying everything she did offscreen in E02. Affects interpretation of her labor, the earlier sale and the distinctions between depicted action, a credit illustration and Naoki's inference. See SPV2-OBS-E06-11 and SPV2-OPEN-E06-04.

### Character and relationship evidence

B’s paired observation and claim proposals. TXT numbers follow the E06 Japanese dialogue index. FRM numbers identify the retained source JPEGs. Times are episode seconds. Recollections describe the speakers’ own earlier states. Audience descriptions of singing remain their testimony.

| Observation and source route | Claim and confidence | Alternative, counterevidence and revision test | Dependents |

| **SPV2-OBS-E06-21** · TXT 1–20, 801–840, 3.180–49.280 and 141.410–215.320; FRM 000014, 000020, 000025, 000043, 000047, 000263, 000284, 000293, 000307, 000314, 000317–000318. Kyoka volunteers, Haru bids, Rio claims only she could do it, with a gold outline at 26.000. Naoki has already selected her. When he proposes removing almost all the dancing from this song, she solicits Haru and Kyoka's objection; both support it on different grounds. High for the contrast and self-blame; moderate for Rio's dominant motive in the first seconds. | A dance-centered group's change of practice is grounds for an artistic objection in its own right. Haru values enjoyment, Kyoka names dance as a means. Neither attributes the decision to Rio's inadequacy. The later request for help and renewed commitment disfavor lasting hostility or simple dislike of the group. | CH-RIO, CH-KYOKA, CH-HARU, CH-NAOKI; REL-RIO-KYOKA, REL-RIO-HARU, REL-HARU-RIO, REL-KYOKA-RIO, REL-NAOKI-RIO. |

### Japanese-language evidence

Release-relative clock. TXT means the selected Japanese fan ASS as numbered in `bundle/dialogue_index.json`. FRM means numbered bundle image or the uncaptioned original picture in `muxed_jp_softsub/shinepost_ep06.jp-softsub.mkv` where indicated. INT means interpretation. I traced the words in the Japanese transcription and sampled the corresponding pictures. No direct listening evidence is asserted.

#### Japanese claims

| ID | Proposed reading | Supporting observations and limit |
|---|---|---|
| SPV2-CLM-E06-31 | Kyōka's readiness to volunteer makes the earlier center conflict into a joke between friends; Rio's apparently still stronger bid creates an obligation she did not expect. | SPV2-OBS-E06-31. INT, high for the reversal. Exact performed hesitation is open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-32 | Rio's agreement to ask Momiji for help is deliberately conditional. Giving the familiar confection and promising success preserve some of the agency she wanted in undertaking practice alone. | SPV2-OBS-E06-32. INT. Native packaging establishes the shop connection to E05, while private motive is a reading. |

### Music and performance evidence

Frozen 2026-11-22. Witness keys follow the [source and intake register](../01%20Source%20and%20Intake/SHINEPOST_V2_SOURCE_AND_INTAKE_REGISTER.md). FRM f#### identifies a retained JPEG by its six-digit leading number in SPV2-W-E06-ZIP. JP identifies SPV2-W-E06-JP at `subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass` in that bundle. AM identifies measurements of the Japanese main stereo programme SPV2-W-E06-AO, PCM-bound to SPV2-W-E06-SRC. Times use the zero-origin programme clock. Paired AM figures give stereo mean-power RMS in dBFS and time-weighted percentage below −45 dBFS within fully contained native 10 ms bins. A low bin may contain sound. Sampled pictures, timed Japanese and programme measurements are distinct witnesses. Individual vocal delivery, continuous movement and source separation remain OPEN.

### Visual claims

| ID | Evidence, source route and limits |
| --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-51 | OBS 51, 52. Rio's early boast is an easier public action than accepting the job she claims. The visual contradiction precedes her verbal explanation. Confidence in some other aspect of herself remains compatible with it. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-52 | OBS 52, 57, 63. The episode uses three kinds of arena or crowd imagery. The opening titles supply a performance ideal, the childhood arena is an attributed memory and the mall supplies witnesses to the current show. A title face cannot identify a mall attendee. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-53 | OBS 53, 54. Rio's repeated return to the door makes the refusal comic while her later memory gives its effect on Momiji weight. Their physical struggle on the street resolves into a one-sided outstretched hand. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-54 | OBS 54, 62. The pudding exchange repairs the broken contact by repeating the reach with a different outcome. The later request and agreement support this reading even if one understands the gift simply as thanks or apology. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-55 | OBS 55, 56, 57. Bringing a friend to Hotaru gives Rio a concrete success at helping someone before she conceives of becoming an idol. She credits the performer for Aoi's smile, so ownership of the outcome remains divided in her mind. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-56 | OBS 56. Position and clothing favor Rio reaching onto the taller companion's resting hand when the crowd unsettles her. The crop prevents an independent facial identification of the bare hand. No family or staff relation follows from its placement. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-57 | OBS 57, 66, 69. Rio's increasing participation among Hotaru's spectators supplies a model for how the mall later depicts spectators being drawn in. Pink arena sticks and yellow mall sticks belong to different venues and events. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-58 | OBS 58, 59. A similar loss of balance has different witnesses at the audition and at the early show. Neither scene measures a later error rate, nor does the small drawn audience establish that Rio personally kept anyone away. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-59 | OBS 51, 59, 60. The remembered invention of the confident posture gives Naoki's interpretation a source outside his own assertion. Rio chooses a visible way to reassure people she believes she burdens. The gesture can still carry pleasure, pride and humor. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-60 | OBS 54, 60, 61. The nightly return makes a cost of independence visible in time spent waiting and in the empty floor she seeks. The swing replay and Naoki's rehearsed-appearance inserts interrupt the present rather than documenting a second street confrontation or farm visit. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-61 | OBS 60, 61. At the point of disclosure, the large floor keeps Naoki at a distance while editing lets us see the partial uncovering of Rio's eyes. His words defer discovery to the concert. This encounter supplies no private acoustic demonstration of her talent. |
| SPV2-CLM-E06-62 | OBS 54, 62. Rio makes both an offer and a request of Momiji. The conditional phrasing leaves the actual dance lesson in the future of the shown encounter. The recognizable shop label links to E05 while the buyer and seller of this particular pot remain open. |

### Open questions and dated consequences

- SPV2-OPEN-E07-01 · Whether, when and under what terms either member returns. Momiji’s willingness if Yukine also returns and Yukine's criticism of Haru do not complete a new five-person membership. A later decision by each and an observed change of activity would bear on SPV2-CLM-E07-05 and SPV2-CLM-E07-11.
- SPV2-OPEN-E07-02 · Why Haru holds something back and precisely what she could show the audience that she has withheld. Her golden assurance, Yukine’s comparative self-assessment and Naoki’s diagnosis have independent routes. They do not supply Haru’s account of the origin, purpose or extent of restraint. See SPV2-CLM-E07-12, SPV2-CLM-E07-13 and SPV2-CLM-E07-14.
- SPV2-OPEN-E07-03 · The complete history behind the pair’s original withdrawal. The memory gives the declaration and its social consequences. The present-day reasons condition current participation. Firsthand evidence tying a past decision to a date and circumstances is needed before backdating them. See SPV2-CLM-E07-07.
- SPV2-OPEN-E07-04 · Musical contents of the mediated clips, recalled concert, dance exercise and playback. The images can identify earlier performances and the return of particular shots. Programme-mix measurements cannot identify an E07 performance of an E02 song, who sings each part, the quality of a voice, how speech interrupts the music or the precise coupling between beat and pose. See SPV2-CLM-E07-02 and SPV2-CLM-E07-13. Main-programme AO/CAV on the matched clocks remains necessary.
- SPV2-OPEN-E07-05 · The degree to which online views, new local fans and past publicity convert to Nakano attendance or other engagements. The deadline is two months away and the office describes continued sharing. Dated sales or a new commission would change the material record. See SPV2-CLM-E07-02 and SPV2-CLM-E07-06.

#### Longitudinal transitions at E07

- SPV2-REV-E07-01 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E06-12 and narrow SPV2-OPEN-E06-05. The E06 last look suggested his next assignment would concern the pair. Naoki now requests that both rejoin the original five. Their independent desires receive different present answers through SPV2-OBS-E07-01, SPV2-OBS-E07-05, SPV2-OBS-E07-11 and SPV2-OBS-E07-12. The original balcony look alone still did not specify that proposal.
- SPV2-REV-E07-02 · STRENGTHEN the confirmed teaching component of SPV2-OPEN-E06-02. Momiji appraises Rio, who asks for additional instruction in SPV2-OBS-E07-03. This supports a change in their directed working relationship while leaving duration, future progress and confidence under different conditions open.
- SPV2-REV-E07-03 · STRENGTHEN the public-reach component of SPV2-OPEN-E06-03 through SPV2-OBS-E07-02. Mall footage circulates and the group reports more fans; pictures place visitors at Bright Station. Nakano sales and any particular passerby’s purchase remain open through SPV2-OPEN-E07-05. This also qualifies the reach established by SPV2-CLM-E03-02.
- SPV2-REV-E07-04 · STRENGTHEN the historical-roster distinction in SPV2-CLM-E01-16 and SPV2-CLM-E02-41. The tablet, two logos and shared memories in SPV2-OBS-E07-02 and SPV2-OBS-E07-05 establish that all five once belonged to TINGS. Neither the E01 studio demonstration nor the E02 public concert has acquired two additional performers.
- SPV2-REV-E07-05 · PRESERVE SPV2-CLM-E01-10, SPV2-CLM-E01-11 and SPV2-CLM-E01-15 with a consequential new instance in SPV2-OBS-E07-14. The sincere dream and her E01 pledges did not confer permanent truthfulness. Her E07 answer about giving full effort receives the gold. This changes the present assessment of Haru and Naoki's understanding of her without retroactively making the E01 aspiration a lie.
- SPV2-REV-E07-06 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E02-43 and PRESERVE the editorial caution of SPV2-CLM-E02-57. The paired rehearsal and concert images in SPV2-OBS-E07-13 show that Naoki has carried the earlier evidence into a new inquiry about unused potential. Rio’s reaching hand before the spectator and Haru's later visible gestures remain separately attributable.

- SPV2-REV-E07-07 · REVISE the source locator for SPV2-OBS-E06-07 and PRESERVE the reading in SPV2-CLM-E06-07. Use `TXT/FRM 697.79–877.44, C985–1044; orig 698.031/699.866/710.001/712.503/713.713/714.005/718.009/724.474/725.808/729.312/744.535/804.804/855.021/871.704` as the source route. All fourteen original-frame samples were independently re-decoded from the hash-matched E06 release and matched the archived picture receipts. The forgotten-object excuse is already in progress by the two gold-outlined pictures at 698.031 and 699.866. The 702.04-second start printed in the E06 acceptance began at the next cue and excluded both pictures it cited. Use the corrected start for the entire meeting-room observation and retain the distinction between the present excuse, Rio’s subsequent boast and the recollected wish for praise. The overlap with SPV2-OBS-E06-06 covers the same excuse from two perspectives. Rio’s decision and the associated character and relationship states are unchanged.

| SPV2-CLM-E07-21 | OBS 21, 27. E06's specifically requested lesson has become active instruction. Rio can seek a verdict and push back on it without surrendering the relationship. Revises E06 CLM 27–29 and REL-RIO-MOMIJI, REL-MOMIJI-RIO, REL-DOORPAIR. | High for the exchange, medium for repeatability under more difficult criticism. A short informal evaluation between rehearsals leaves the formality and duration of instruction open. Rio's self-report of earlier coaching strengthens continuity while complicating any claim of a first-ever lesson. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-22 | OBS 22, 23. A documented former five turns the E06 gallery glance into a retrospective lead. The new proposal belongs to Naoki. His criterion concerns all members using their abilities, not simply occupying five positions. CH-NAOKI, CH-YUKI, REL-NAOKI-TRIO, REL-YUKI-NAOKI. | High for original membership and immediate proposal, lower for when he settled on it. Neither the archive nor the graphic identifies a fully known cause of departure. A later dating of his decision or evidence of what the documents contained could change that inference. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-23 | OBS 22–24, 28. Haru's remembrance motivates the effort to reunite. The withdrawal created a knowledge gap that Rio and Kyoka try to fill by independent self-blame. Haru keeps them from treating these guesses as established. CH-HARU, CH-RIO, CH-KYOKA, REL-HARU-YUKINE. | High for asymmetry, medium for memory's causal weight beside the concert deadline. Yukine's present reason to withhold agreement is specific. It need not exhaust the reason for the original exit. An explicit dated account from a departing member would revise the past reconstruction. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-24 | OBS 23–24. Rio tests a personal fear under cover of a recruitment visit. Yukine gives her practical care and a direct answer which the earlier group meeting lacked. CH-RIO, CH-DOOR-COMPANION, REL-RIO-YUKINE, REL-YUKINE-RIO. | High for what each asks or gives. Yukine could also want to spare Rio's feelings. No contradiction of her direct denial is supplied. A fuller departure account could establish an indirect group factor without making Rio personally responsible. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-25 | OBS 25. Kyoka argues from the value of covering each other and immediately receives such attention from a woman retaining her freedom to decline. The familiar joke and later quarrel over stubbornness preserve Kyoka's ordinary self-presentation. CH-KYOKA, CH-DOOR-COMPANION, REL-KYOKA-YUKINE, REL-YUKINE-KYOKA, REL-RIO-KYOKA. | High for the concrete help and refusal. Moderate for enduring personal solicitude since anyone might stop a runner over an unsafe lace. Combined with other individually tailored acts it is stronger evidence than a general assertion of affection. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-26 | OBS 26–27. Kei converts an errand into a bid for time with Naoki. Haru converts assistance already received into an inducement. The two exchanges establish different requests and let the recipients answer back. CH-KEI, CH-NAOKI, CH-HARU, REL-KEI-NAOKI, REL-NAOKI-KEI, REL-HARU-YUKINE. | High for offers and corrections. A genuine appetite or attraction to the photograph can coexist with wanting company. No purchase by Naoki or deliberately staged lack of money by Haru is established. A receipt, meeting or admission would date a further claim. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-27 | OBS 21, 22, 26, 27. The recorded yes defeats Momiji's denial of saying it but the ensuing conditions qualify its meaning. Her commitment to Yukine is one she explains and maintains while she can still want Rio's company. CH-MOMIJI, REL-MOMIJI-COMPANION, REL-MOMIJI-RIO, REL-KYOKA-MOMIJI, REL-HARU-MOMIJI. | High for the stated condition. Medium for the extent of any prior settled desire to rejoin. A spontaneous answer, a concession under pressure and a hypothetical with both partners need different treatment. An independent unconditional choice could change her current position. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-28 | OBS 21, 23–28. Haru sees repeated attention and makes an earnest personal invitation. Yukine treats Haru's withheld potential and her own relative standing as obstacles, complicating the generic rival rationale. CH-DOOR-COMPANION, CH-HARU, REL-HARU-YUKINE, REL-YUKINE-HARU. | High for their incompatible accounts of the return, medium for how much rivalry and care contribute to attendance. The hand is an invitation without a completed clasp. Future consent, a stated departure motive, or a distinction between past and current motives would change different parts of the claim. |
| SPV2-CLM-E07-29 | OBS 21, 28, 29. Yukine's challenge receives an independent professional judgment grounded in Naoki's account of watching Haru. Her blanket reassurance is visually marked for the first time in this exchange. Her reason for holding back stays undisclosed. CH-HARU, CH-NAOKI, REL-NAOKI-HARU, REL-HARU-NAOKI. | High for judgments, hesitation and gold, moderate for the actual range of her unused capacity. Effort spent meeting the group's needs can be real. A later explanation of what she counts as full effort or an independently assessable performance could distinguish the alternatives. The other girls are present without evidence that they perceive the effect. |

### Japanese observations and claims

TXT refers to the selected Japanese fan ASS as numbered in `bundle/dialogue_index.json`. FRM refers to native picture, including independent uncaptioned samples from `muxed_jp_softsub/shinepost_ep07.jp-softsub.mkv`, or to bundle frames by their six-digit IDs. INT marks interpretation of the exchange. Times are E07-relative. The transcription has no speaker labels for these cues. Performed voices, isolated delivery, and sung-part allocation remain open.

### Music and performance observations and claims

Frozen 2026-11-22. Keys follow the [source and intake register](../01%20Source%20and%20Intake/SHINEPOST_V2_SOURCE_AND_INTAKE_REGISTER.md). FRM f#### identifies an image by its six-digit leading number within SPV2-W-E07-ZIP. JP identifies SPV2-W-E07-JP, `subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass`. AM measures SPV2-W-E07-AO, the Japanese main stereo programme PCM-matched to SPV2-W-E07-SRC. All times are programme-relative. Paired measurements give mean-power RMS in dBFS and the percentage of retained time below −45 dBFS, measured over fully contained 10 ms bins. Direct listening (AO) and continuous-motion/source-synchrony review (CAV) remain OPEN. The duo comprises SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION (Gionji Yukine) and SPV2-CH-MOMIJI (Ito Momiji). The active trio comprises SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-CH-KYOKA and SPV2-CH-RIO. Their manager is SPV2-CH-NAOKI and the agency head SPV2-CH-YUKI. Short OBS/CLM references below carry the episode prefix SPV2-OBS-E07 or SPV2-CLM-E07.

### Principal claims

| Claim | Inference and supporting evidence | Counterevidence and revision conditions |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-01 | INT. The mother lets an actor see a spectator’s experience before she must play somebody who sings to spectators. SPV2-OBS-E08-01. | The invitation is what we see. Her mother need not have intended Yukine to take up an additional profession. Her later account of the arena supplies the link. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-02 | INF. Practical authority begins with a vacancy and voluntary assent by the other four. SPV2-OBS-E08-02. | The president gives tools and sets a professional ambition. She does not name a leader in this conversation. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-03 | INF. Her advice is differentiated, but her account of Haru’s difficulty assumes a novice biography. SPV2-OBS-E08-03. | Haru's earlier group is revealed at the end of E08. Yukine lacked the information when she spoke. Her advice need not have been consciously imposed or universally ineffective. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-04 | INT. The five begin to share instructional authority. Particular skills and the need for help move between members. SPV2-OBS-E08-04. | A character diagnoses lateness in reproducing the choreography. Measuring actual beat error would require synchronized continuous evidence. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-05 | INT. Front-of-house work costs Yukine the effort of repairing both an encounter and practical errors in publicity. SPV2-OBS-E08-05. | Audience embarrassment is inferred from expression and Yukine's intervention. Neither a lost sale nor a correction to the printed run is shown. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-06 | INT. Private confidence permits a public leader to remain theatrical and gives the duo its name while the five still work together. SPV2-OBS-E08-06. | Momiji does not announce Yukine’s secret to the trio. Their surprise the next morning concerns names and renewed energy, not proof that they know what was said. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-07 | INF. Yukine first tests her teaching as a reason for a plateau and makes a safer occasion for disclosure than an accusation would. SPV2-OBS-E08-07. | Haru's reassurance and gratitude do not disclose her training history. Yukine had some evidence of genuine progress elsewhere in the group. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-08 | INT. The familiar Hotaru performance now answers a different spectator's question. Haru's ambition also locates an achievement in the freedom of her audience. SPV2-OBS-E08-08. | Reuse securely identifies the pictured performer and event imagery. It cannot identify E08's sounding song or establish how much Momiji overheard. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-09 | INF. Finishing together and still needing rehearsal are compatible. The actual help is evidence against Yukine’s later wholesale dismissal of her leadership. SPV2-OBS-E08-09. | The five finishing says little about Haru's maximum, and the latter part of the evening does not document a perfect run or how often they rehearsed. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-10 | INT. Momiji’s observational skill and her reluctance to interfere coexist. Suspicion precedes the sight of the rooftop solo. SPV2-OBS-E08-10. | The look cannot identify exactly what she noticed, when her suspicion began, or whether she had already seen comparable movement elsewhere. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-11 | INF. The gap between the familiar teammate and the girl they see performing alone changes what Yukine can believe about her own lessons. SPV2-OBS-E08-11. | The camera offers positions unavailable through the door. No reliable quantitative estimate of hidden ability or specific vocal accomplishment follows from the pictures. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-12 | INF. The long wait for words includes a particularly low-level approach followed by a substantial change in the mix. SPV2-OBS-E08-12. | Low-level mixed sound is not necessarily silence. A credit or device title elsewhere cannot assign music, breath or steps to individual changes. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-13 | INT. Yukine confuses an error about Haru with a more sweeping judgment about what she gave the other four. The president offers a theory of protection. SPV2-OBS-E08-13. | The others earlier thanked the leader. C1054's wording in this transcript is problematic. C1055–1057 independently establish the comparison and hypothetical harm. No prior injury is named. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-14 | INF. A deliberate agency-backed attempt to provoke protective action helped cause the split the other three could not explain in E07. SPV2-OBS-E08-14. | Yukine also feels angry and humiliated. Knowing the strategy does not reduce her whole motive to compliance or verify that the proposed intervention works. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-15 | INT. Real work underlies the assumed rivalry. Each girl has to watch the three become a public group without her. SPV2-OBS-E08-15. | Neither pictures of loads nor narrated endurance quantify how close the pair came to Haru. Visual reuse identifies the historical staging, not an E08 audible replay. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-16 | INF. Haru supplies her own fear to the group. The strongest marked proposition evaluates what she ought to do. SPV2-OBS-E08-16. | The effect says nothing specific about any former member, victim or extent of harm. It need not invalidate the separately spoken fear. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-17 | INT. The trio’s responses diverge. Kyoka can recognize the pair’s history of care before Rio or anyone else can explain Haru’s. SPV2-OBS-E08-17. | Kyoka's rebuttal does not undo the actual separation. The new roster and willingness of all five remain undecided. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-18 | INF. Editing strongly identifies HY:RAIN as the association the episode wants a viewer to make. Former group membership is verbally explicit. SPV2-OBS-E08-18. | The name is written on the poster, not spoken in C1147. The pictured five do not include the passing Haru. Dates, rank and cause of departure are unestablished. |

SPV2-OBS-E08-19. | These are production and framing evidence. Who sang which syllable, the recording played on the roof and a restored five in the present remain distinct questions. |

### Open questions and dated consequences

- SPV2-OPEN-E08-01 · Haru’s former group. SPV2-OBS-E08-18 supplies verbal confirmation of membership somewhere and a strong visual identification with HY:RAIN. The dates, working relationships, circumstances of departure and connection with present fear need an account from Haru, an identified other participant or a dated source. Affects SPV2-CH-HARU and SPV2-REL-HARU-YUKINE.
- SPV2-OPEN-E08-02 · Harm as expectation or history. Haru names her fear of hurting everyone. Yuki describes a possible effect on the original TINGS. Are they talking about a past experience, a prediction, or both? Can anyone independently attest an actual past injury? See SPV2-CLM-E08-13, SPV2-CLM-E08-16, SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-CH-YUKI and SPV2-REL-NAOKI-HARU.
- SPV2-OPEN-E08-03 · Terms of performing together. The pair’s intervention had uncertain prospects from the start. After the direct appeal fails, nobody accepts a new lineup. What further choices are made, by whom, under what terms, and with what effect on the show and the Nakano deadline? SPV2-OPEN-E07-01 and SPV2-OPEN-E07-05 remain relevant.
- SPV2-OPEN-E08-04 · Acoustic and continuous movement evidence. Which music, if any, accompanies which stretch of Haru’s solo? How do counted rehearsal, Hotaru's arena, the later reprises and the solitary dance relate to actual phrasing, steps or breath? Do successive five-person rehearsals sound different? The explicit player and production credits do not by themselves answer these questions. See SPV2-CLM-E08-03, SPV2-CLM-E08-04, SPV2-CLM-E08-11, SPV2-CLM-E08-12 and SPV2-CLM-E08-19.
- SPV2-OPEN-E08-05 · Knowledge before the door opened. Momiji voiced suspicion before she saw the sunset solo. Which concrete earlier observation led her to the categorical “never once”? What did Yuki personally witness, and when did she learn of the earlier membership? The fireworks glance, the president’s expertise and her arrival at the door do not individually settle those histories. See SPV2-OBS-E08-10, SPV2-OBS-E08-11, SPV2-OBS-E08-13 and SPV2-OBS-E08-18.

#### Longitudinal transitions at E08

- SPV2-REV-E08-01 · STRENGTHEN the factual basis of SPV2-CLM-E07-07 and REVISE the open status of the departure strategy in SPV2-OPEN-E07-03. SPV2-OBS-E08-13 and SPV2-OBS-E08-14 add the proposal, its two-sided risk and implementation. E07 f0611/f0624 and E08 f1053/f1057 show Haru looking up and the same five figures outside the agency. Consequences for SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION, SPV2-CH-MOMIJI, SPV2-CH-YUKI, SPV2-REL-YUKINE-HARU, SPV2-REL-MOMIJI-HARU and SPV2-PF-E07-06. The strategy and its consequences for friendship have different levels of success.
- SPV2-REV-E08-02 · STRENGTHEN the observed disparity in SPV2-CLM-E07-12 and SPV2-CLM-E07-13 through SPV2-OBS-E08-03 and SPV2-OBS-E08-11. The younger Yukine assessed unobtrusive competence, then saw the much more striking solitary performance. Her comparative judgment is firsthand within those conditions. No precise unused fraction or direct comparison of individual singing follows. Affects SPV2-REL-YUKINE-HARU, SPV2-CH-HARU and SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION.
- SPV2-REV-E08-03 · REVISE the scope of SPV2-OPEN-E07-02 while preserving SPV2-CLM-E07-14. Haru’s fear is now explicitly shared through SPV2-OBS-E08-16, while her earlier group becomes explicit through SPV2-OBS-E08-18. The proposition that identifies an actual harm or names its cause remains OPEN in SPV2-OPEN-E08-01 and SPV2-OPEN-E08-02. The continuing ability to identify a marked utterance changes what Naoki can ask, not what he already knew. See SPV2-CH-NAOKI and SPV2-REL-NAOKI-HARU.
- SPV2-REV-E08-04 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E07-11. SPV2-OBS-E08-06 dates the name Yukimoji and Momiji's refusal to leave anybody alone earlier than the professional rivalry. Affects SPV2-REL-DOORPAIR, SPV2-REL-MOMIJI-COMPANION, SPV2-CH-MOMIJI and SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION. That history helps explain a present choice without converting Momiji’s conditional willingness in E07 into consent to rejoin alone.
- SPV2-REV-E08-05 · STRENGTHEN the attested history of Haru’s wish in SPV2-OBS-E01-18 through SPV2-OBS-E08-08. She describes wanting people to proclaim the idol they love while the original five are still together. PRESERVE SPV2-CLM-E01-11 in its specific context of Naoki’s later decision to try management. Her restraint before the current trio and her earlier aspiration have independent evidence. See SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-REL-HARU-YUKINE and SPV2-REL-NAOKI-HARU.
- SPV2-REV-E08-06 · STRENGTHEN the material history of care in SPV2-CLM-E07-08 and SPV2-CLM-E07-12. Kyoka explicitly argues for continuity despite real withdrawal. SPV2-OBS-E08-17 identifies earlier backstage, roadside and balcony examples with independently matched pictures from E04 and E06. Keep Kyoka’s argument distinct from Rio’s unanswered question. See SPV2-REL-KYOKA-YUKINE, SPV2-REL-MOMIJI-RIO, SPV2-REL-RIO-MOMIJI and SPV2-REL-KYOKA-MOMIJI.

### Character claims, dissent and change conditions

| Claim | Interpretation and dependents | Alternative, confidence and evidence that would change it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-21 | OBS 21. The mother's intervention supplies an occasion for Yukine to discover an ambition beyond her role. Yukine later gives the experience her own meaning. CH-DOOR-COMPANION, CH-YUKINE-MOTHER, CH-HOTARU, REL-YUKINE-HOTARU. | High for the catalyst and aspiration. Learning to play a role is the mother's stated purpose. A deliberate maternal plan for a career change would need a separate account. No meeting with Hotaru or another principal spectator is shown here. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-22 | OBS 22–23, 25. The early Yukine offers precise, transferable help, delegates to a dancer with more expertise and misjudges one performer. This dates E07's remembered leadership without guaranteeing perfect diagnosis. CH-DOOR-COMPANION, CH-MOMIJI, REL-DOORPAIR, REL-YUKINE-RIO, REL-YUKINE-KYOKA. | High for actions, medium for the source of her judgment. Previous acting plausibly helps but she expressly disclaims prior idol experience. Later evidence of technical training would refine that connection. A completed dance is not proof of lasting equality of skill. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-23 | OBS 23–24. Becoming the practical remedy for other people's errors costs Yukine effort that her peers notice. “Ogre” and the fan-service joke show that gratitude allows irreverence too. CH-KYOKA, CH-RIO, CH-HARU, CH-DOOR-COMPANION, REL-KYOKA-YUKINE. | High for reliance and spoken appreciation. Yukine treats the work as useful idol experience and takes initiative freely. Chronic resentment or burnout would require separate evidence across contexts. A customer apology does not identify which girl printed the date. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-24 | OBS 24, 27–28. Momiji notices both the performed persona and the real service it provides. “Yukimoji” initially names a collaboration inside the five. The later break changes its organizational meaning. CH-MOMIJI, CH-DOOR-COMPANION, REL-DOORPAIR, REL-MOMIJI-COMPANION. | High. An incidental naming joke alone would not support the claim, but the request for help and change to given names establish a concrete consequence. The trio see new familiarity without necessarily knowing the private disclosure. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-25 | OBS 21, 25–26. Early closeness between Yukine and Haru includes soliciting criticism and exchanging actual professional ideals. Haru's reassurance leaves a gap in Yukine's account of their work. CH-HARU, CH-DOOR-COMPANION, REL-HARU-YUKINE, REL-YUKINE-HARU. | High for what they chose to share, medium for Haru's intent in withholding more. Gratitude can be sincere even if their estimates of her ability conflict. One sideways look at fireworks cannot date when Momiji learned about her dance. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-26 | OBS 25–27. Momiji's judgment precedes their joint sighting and changes Yukine's knowledge. Yukine experiences the newly visible discrepancy as a breach of a shared promise. CH-MOMIJI, CH-DOOR-COMPANION, REL-MOMIJI-HARU, REL-MOMIJI-COMPANION. | High for order and Yukine's reaction. Neither the first basis of Momiji's suspicion nor every earlier instance of deliberate restraint is shown. New evidence of earlier contact or confidence from Haru would revise Momiji's knowledge. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-27 | OBS 26–28. Yuki originated the risky rival strategy. Yukine's wounded pride, wish to elicit Haru's strength, loyalty to the five and consent to risk all matter. Momiji accepts participation and training. Revises E07 CLM 23/28, CH-YUKI, CH-NANAMI, REL-YUKI-YUKINE, REL-YUKI-MOMIJI. | High for recommendation, execution and understood lack of guarantee. A protective interpretation by Yuki is not a complete personal history supplied by Haru. Both actual strategic rivalry and retained care survive. Evidence that Yuki knew a specific previous injury would narrow her source of knowledge. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-28 | OBS 27–29. Haru gives a public reason for refusing and withdraws, partly disconfirming an expectation that she would require privacy to say even this much. The gold underlines conflict in her categorical judgment about what she should do. Yukine experiences failure and Momiji offers a distinct measure of their effort. CH-HARU, CH-MOMIJI, CH-DOOR-COMPANION, REL-HARU-YUKINE, REL-MOMIJI-COMPANION, REL-NAOKI-HARU. | High for utterances, effect and departure. The warning about possible hurt may be a real fear. The effect need not invalidate all earlier words or identify who was harmed. A named event or Haru's own account could explain the fear. No evidence of the others seeing the gold. |
| SPV2-CLM-E08-29 | OBS 23, 27–29. Kyoka recognizes care that compromised the attempt to seem estranged. Rio can share that history and still have a real question about causation. Yuki begins a distinct disclosure about Haru after Haru leaves. CH-KYOKA, CH-RIO, CH-YUKI, REL-KYOKA-YUKINE, REL-RIO-YUKINE, REL-YUKI-TRIO. | High for earlier membership, high for the visual suggestion of HY:RAIN, unresolved for the specific prior events. The poster supplies a name the dialogue withholds. Direct naming would strengthen identification. The full office briefing cannot be assigned wholesale to the rooftop listeners. |

### Japanese observations and claims

C numbers are chronological `bundle/dialogue_index.json` entries. ASS Dialogue ordinal is a one-based count of rendered Dialogue records in physical file order, ignoring Comment records. The original bilingual JPSC/JPTC and selected Japanese ASS require different ordinals. Times are relative to 00:00.000 on the 1371.040-second release. TXT denotes fan transcription or its sibling translation, FRM original lettering or visible scene evidence, and INT interpretation. Original picture was sampled without subtitle overlays. No independent listening.

### Music and performance evidence

Frozen 2026-11-22. Keys follow the [source and intake register](../01%20Source%20and%20Intake/SHINEPOST_V2_SOURCE_AND_INTAKE_REGISTER.md). FRM f#### identifies the JPG by its six-digit prefix in SPV2-W-E08-ZIP. Its printed clock is the manifest request time unless designated source PTS. JP is SPV2-W-E08-JP, `subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass`. “Cue” means the chronological `dialogue_index.csv` index; “ASS event” means the physical order of Dialogue records alone. AM measures the PCM-verified Japanese main stereo SPV2-W-E08-AO, bound to SPV2-W-E08-SRC. Paired values are programme RMS in dBFS and the percentage below −45 dBFS in fully contained native 10 ms bins. All times are zero-origin. Direct listening (AO), continuous motion and audio/visual synchronization (CAV) remain OPEN. Local OBS/CLM suffixes below take the prefix SPV2-OBS-E08 or SPV2-CLM-E08. Yukine is SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION, Momiji SPV2-CH-MOMIJI. Haru, Kyoka, Rio, Hotaru, Yuki, Naoki and Nanami retain the corresponding SPV2-CH-HARU, -KYOKA, -RIO, -HOTARU, -YUKI, -NAOKI and -NANAMI identifiers.
