### Principal claims

| Claim | Inference and supporting evidence | Counterevidence and revision conditions |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-01 | INF. Haru can know the people as a former member while occupying the same physical position as any spectator of their present fame. SPV2-OBS-E09-01. | The feature’s statements are attributed publicity. Success has no bearing on whether Ren welcomed Haru’s departure. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-02 | INT. The aspiration has a collective origin. Ren and Haru enter as friends, and training gives them real gains to set beside subsequent comparisons. SPV2-OBS-E09-02. | Homare’s design matches the schoolfriend addressed by name in E02; her E09 credit is in f1335. No cue says she also auditioned. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-03 | INF. The evaluation makes a difference in ability publicly legible within the room, after earlier one-to-one encouragement. SPV2-OBS-E09-03. | Visible interest and Haru’s account support unusual talent. The formal result, criteria and votes are unspecified. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-04 | INT. A distressing comparison becomes evidence to Haru for a general rule. The words do not establish that Ren resented Haru or wanted her to withdraw. SPV2-OBS-E09-04. | Ren earlier praised and challenged Haru. Her own account of the test, the departure and their friendship could alter that reading. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-05 | INF. Quitting is both a response to a friend’s visible pain and an attempt to spare herself a recurrence of that experience. SPV2-OBS-E09-05. | Only Haru names the reason. No expulsion, discussion between the friends or statement of Ren’s wishes is shown. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-06 | INT. The limitation begins as a term negotiated with the president. It binds later teammates who were not parties to that negotiation. SPV2-OBS-E09-06. | Yuki’s attendance at the performance does not make her a witness inside the locker room. She identifies neither a competence threshold nor its arbiter. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-07 | INF. Haru’s firsthand narrative belongs to the episode’s viewer; the current group hears the president’s telling. Its four responses have different targets. SPV2-OBS-E09-07. | Which confidential details Yuki knew independently and how Haru told her about Ren are left out. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-08 | INF. Kyoka redirects an agency-backed rival strategy into a jointly undertaken public obligation, with disbandment still a real stake. SPV2-OBS-E09-08. | Her prediction about needing Haru’s full effort is her conditional argument, not a new contract observed on screen. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-09 | INT. The practical reason for the song is recoverable before the final performance: existing common work addresses a genuine problem of rearrangement. SPV2-OBS-E09-09. | Working out once how to finish together cannot guarantee readiness after separation, or Haru’s attendance. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-10 | INF. The episode supplies a concrete environmental clue for the manager’s apparently impressive inference. SPV2-OBS-E09-10. | No hearing of the announcement, channel placement, volume or individual perceptual acuity is claimed. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-11 | INF. He offers Haru firsthand information about himself which lets him ask her to reconsider by analogy. Her earlier words had already helped him. SPV2-OBS-E09-11. | He names neither all the people hurt nor their specific injuries. Earlier testimony supplies other perspectives on his professionalism. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-12 | INT. Reciprocity gives his request weight. The visible departure leaves a period in which Haru can decide for herself. SPV2-OBS-E09-12. | The concert is going ahead and the group’s future matters to her. The choice carries pressure even without an escort. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-13 | INF. The girls’ achievements and resistance are evidence about the very people she presumed unable to bear her full effort. SPV2-OBS-E09-13. | Some inserted views reproduce scenes Haru could not have seen from that angle. The point at which she decided and her activity during the intervening time remain open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-14 | INF. The intended beneficiaries of Haru’s restraint contest the judgment made on their behalf. Their willingness and technical equality are separate propositions. SPV2-OBS-E09-14. | Seeing her arrive does not establish her full resolve at the door. The post-show reactions complicate the boast of superiority. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-15 | INF. A public MC can contain a promise to a particular person without briefing the room on her private history. SPV2-OBS-E09-15. | Fans respond visibly, but none explains that she recognized Haru’s past or heard a private meaning. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-16 | INT. The concert preserves a shape developed with five in rehearsal and makes it visible to fans after the members have put their trust to a test. SPV2-OBS-E09-16. | The printed lyric and a five-person vocal credit cannot assign individual voices or verify footwork between samples. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-17 | INT. The programme shows and identifies the first number far more fully than the next. Post-concert fatigue may reflect work beyond Be Your Light!!. SPV2-OBS-E09-17. | A title and an ellipsis do not establish the full set list, choreography or sounding duration of the next number. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-18 | INF. Four distinct answers to an actual disparity give Haru a better basis for trust than an assurance of equality could have done. SPV2-OBS-E09-18. | They have only completed this live. The vows, the next training and future Nakano success are prospective. |

SPV2-OBS-E09-19. | The credits supply a separate composition. The encore request does not identify an encore number or certify another performance. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-20 | INT. The credits bring old and present relationships into one frame of remembrance; billing provides a separate witness to performers and works. SPV2-OBS-E09-20. | The image behind a card does not establish which piece was played on that roof. Present-day action with Ren remains unshown. |

### Open questions and consequences at the E09 boundary

- SPV2-OPEN-E09-01 · Ren's own account. The evaluation, tears and wish to equal Haru are witnessed. Her response to Haru leaving and her present feelings toward her former teammate remain open. A direct encounter, quoted exchange or identified contemporaneous witness could strengthen or overturn the causal story Haru tells herself. Affects SPV2-CLM-E09-04, SPV2-CLM-E09-05 and SPV2-CH-HARU.
- SPV2-OPEN-E09-02 · The cost of the resumed five. Kyoka issues an order, four undertake the live, and Haru agrees through action. How do they share practice, redistribute parts and handle a disparity after this evening? A later public or private rehearsal, concert or explicit agreement could test the extent of their change. Affects SPV2-CLM-E09-08, SPV2-CLM-E09-14, SPV2-CLM-E09-18, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-HARU and SPV2-CH-RIO. Nakano and disbandment remain unresolved.
- SPV2-OPEN-E09-03 · Performance form and sound. What is audible under the HY:RAIN feature, center test, park announcement on the phone and live MC? Where do the TINGS song and subsequent number begin or end in sound? How are the verse, calls and overlapping phrases actually divided? Review complete corresponding Japanese release intervals with direct hearing and continuous movement, preserving the transcript discrepancy at 1109.30–1114.19. Affects SPV2-CLM-E09-09, SPV2-CLM-E09-10, SPV2-CLM-E09-15, SPV2-CLM-E09-16, SPV2-CLM-E09-17 and SPV2-CH-RIO.
- SPV2-OPEN-E09-04 · Whose account reached the rooftop. Yuki is identifiable among the observers of the center test and personally negotiated Haru’s return. The episode does not establish her presence at the locker room, which details Haru had conveyed to her, or exactly what the remaining four learned about Ren. A second witness or a direct exchange could resolve those knowledge limits. Affects SPV2-CLM-E09-06, SPV2-CLM-E09-07, SPV2-CH-YUKI and SPV2-REL-NAOKI-HARU.
- SPV2-OPEN-E09-05 · Divergent careers. Current HY:RAIN has a named five-person musical billing; memories place Haru and four other girls in training. The center verdict, dates of later personnel changes, who learned of her reason for leaving and how the old friends regard one another now are unestablished. The televised career summary has limited value for any of those questions. Affects SPV2-CLM-E09-01, SPV2-CLM-E09-03, SPV2-CLM-E09-20 and SPV2-CH-HARU.
- SPV2-OPEN-E09-06 · Naoki’s earlier workplace. He volunteers what he feared repeating. His testimony does not identify every person he means, how the relationships ended or how those people understood the outcome. Revisit SPV2-CLM-E01-07 and SPV2-CLM-E09-11 if identified colleagues or independently dated accounts become available. Affects SPV2-CH-NAOKI, SPV2-REL-NAOKI-HARU and SPV2-REL-HARU-NAOKI.

### Longitudinal transitions at E09

- SPV2-REV-E09-01 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E08-18 and REVISE SPV2-OPEN-E08-01. Rio now explicitly names HY:RAIN after Yuki’s account. Haru recalls the earlier five, their training and her decision to leave. The present press profile describes a subsequent lineup. SPV2-OBS-E09-01, SPV2-OBS-E09-02, SPV2-OBS-E09-05 and SPV2-OBS-E09-07. Affects SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-CH-YUKI, SPV2-CH-REN, SPV2-REL-HARU-REN and SPV2-REL-REN-HARU. Precise casting and Ren’s current position toward Haru recur in SPV2-OPEN-E09-01 and SPV2-OPEN-E09-05.
- SPV2-REV-E09-02 · STRENGTHEN the historical basis for SPV2-CLM-E08-16 through SPV2-OBS-E09-03, SPV2-OBS-E09-04 and SPV2-OBS-E09-06. Ren’s distress supplies a past incident with identified participants. Haru’s conclusion that her talent caused harm and her request to withhold her full effort are explicit. DOWNGRADE any broader reading of SPV2-OPEN-E08-02 that would treat Ren as asking her to withdraw. That proposition remains unsupported. Affects SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-CH-REN, SPV2-REL-HARU-REN, SPV2-REL-REN-HARU and SPV2-REL-HARU-NAOKI.
- SPV2-REV-E09-03 · REVISE SPV2-OPEN-E08-03, preserving SPV2-CLM-E08-14. The pair’s rival strategy failed to elicit Haru’s full effort on its original terms. Kyoka ends the strategy, asks Yukine and Momiji back and brings the four into a public undertaking. Haru subsequently joins them. SPV2-OBS-E09-08, SPV2-OBS-E09-09, SPV2-OBS-E09-15 and SPV2-OBS-E09-18. Affects SPV2-CH-KYOKA, SPV2-CH-MOMIJI, SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION and SPV2-REL-KYOKA-HARU. Retain SPV2-OPEN-E09-02 for Nakano and future relations.
- SPV2-REV-E09-04 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E01-07 and SPV2-CLM-E01-11 with Naoki's own address to Haru in SPV2-OBS-E09-11 and SPV2-OBS-E09-12. His experience of harm explains the prohibition, and her words about fans gave him hope that he might manage again. Preserve earlier witnesses’ own perspectives. His invitation now places him in the requesting position. Affects SPV2-CH-NAOKI, SPV2-REL-NAOKI-HARU and SPV2-REL-HARU-NAOKI.
- SPV2-REV-E09-05 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E08-09 through SPV2-OBS-E09-09, SPV2-OBS-E09-16 and SPV2-OBS-E09-17. Kyoka identifies the first five’s shared rehearsal as the reason for choosing this song and explains publicly why the concert matters. A rehearsal image and a public formation correspond at native 565.023 and 1123.039. Preserve the earlier observation of imperfections and SPV2-OPEN-E08-04 for the sound of the earlier performance. Affects SPV2-CH-KYOKA, SPV2-CH-RIO and the dated performance records.
- SPV2-REV-E09-06 · REVISE the extent of the uncertainty in SPV2-OPEN-E08-05. Yuki stands among the onlookers at the center test (E09 retained f0367) and says she saw Haru’s performance. Her independent access to the locker-room encounter and the source of Momiji’s earlier suspicion remain open. SPV2-OBS-E09-03, SPV2-OBS-E09-06 and SPV2-OPEN-E09-04. Affects SPV2-CH-MOMIJI and SPV2-CH-YUKI.

| Claim | Interpretation and dependents | Confidence, alternative and revision route |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-21 | SPV2-OBS-E09-21. Shared pleasure and rivalry precede the rupture. Ren's pride in Haru appears in a practice exchange and in ordinary school life that includes Homare. Earlier five established as Haru, Ren, Yawara, Itoha and Aoba. SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-CH-REN, SPV2-CH-HOMARE, SPV2-REL-REN-HARU, SPV2-REL-HARU-REN. | High for inclusion and Ren's explicit praise. Present rankings are a broadcaster's report. The other three's one reaction shot does not give each a private opinion. The later cast card also bills 氷海菜花 but Haru does not include her among the earlier five. A later named history could date any lineup change. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-22 | SPV2-OBS-E09-21, SPV2-OBS-E09-22. Ren's sadness is firsthand evidence for Haru. The general causal rule that full performance hurts those around her is Haru's interpretation of what she saw and heard. Revises SPV2-CLM-E08-28 and SPV2-CLM-E08-29 and SPV2-CH-REN, SPV2-CH-HARU. | High for distress, wish, departure and subsequent distance. Hurt at a perceived gap can coexist with admiration and the wish to improve. Nobody expels Haru in this account, no selection outcome is announced, and neither malice nor Ren's wish to lose her friend follows. Ren's direct retrospective account could discriminate. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-23 | SPV2-OBS-E09-22, SPV2-OBS-E09-23. Yuki has direct access to the center trial and to Haru's eventual condition for returning. E08's partial account of protective restraint now has a historical agreement and an expiry criterion. SPV2-CH-YUKI, SPV2-REL-YUKI-HARU, SPV2-REL-HARU-YUKI, SPV2-REL-YUKI-TRIO. | High. The other girls did not negotiate their own readiness. Yuki was present at the trial but is absent from the pictured locker and corridor. Her briefing need not communicate every thought in the first-person passage or explain how she learned every detail. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-24 | SPV2-OBS-E09-23, SPV2-OBS-E09-24, SPV2-OBS-E09-25. Four people with different reservations convert the dangerous rival wager into an alternative test as teammates. Kyoka sets the initiative and supplies the feasible song; Naoki names the concert as the setting, Rio stress-tests the proposal. SPV2-REL-KYOKA-YUKINE, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-MOMIJI, SPV2-REL-RIO-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-TRIO-NAOKI. | High for proposal and accepted return, moderate for Kyoka's standing to end either act by fiat. The breakup is her predicted consequence, while the two initially call out the unilateral manner. The original arrangement gives them prior choreography but cannot guarantee a successful live with no current five-person rehearsal. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-25 | SPV2-OBS-E09-23, SPV2-OBS-E09-24, SPV2-OBS-E09-25. Naoki earns a hearing through a reciprocal disclosure, invokes a request Haru once made of him and then leaves her the choice. Reinterprets E01 SPV2-REL-HARU-NAOKI and SPV2-REL-NAOKI-HARU. | High for his own account and departure. The prior injuries from his work could differ substantially from Ren's distress. No names, equivalence between incidents or proof of how his former clients judged him follows. Practical responsibility for the group and personal hope can coexist. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-26 | SPV2-OBS-E09-24, SPV2-OBS-E09-25, SPV2-OBS-E09-26, SPV2-OBS-E09-27. Haru is offered reasons to revise a generic judgment of weakness one person at a time. Each given example comes from what she can now identify about a partner. SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-REL-HARU-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-HARU-RIO, SPV2-REL-HARU-YUKINE, SPV2-REL-HARU-MOMIJI. | High for her articulated reasoning, moderate for a settled decision at this point. Her thought breaks off as a possibility. The posted lineup, fans' expectations and her later appearance establish public anticipation and participation, not a precisely timed inward conversion. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-27 | SPV2-OBS-E09-24, SPV2-OBS-E09-26, SPV2-OBS-E09-27, SPV2-OBS-E09-28. Kyoka's public trust request uses the five-person song as evidence the group can answer Haru in her own medium. The two arrivals face an audience for the first time in this formation. SPV2-CH-KYOKA, SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-HARU, SPV2-REL-FANS-TRIO. | High for words, actions and venue. Haru's attendance is compatible with residual anxiety, evident backstage before and after. The audience need not know the Ren story to receive the song. Captions and stills do not establish audibly superior singing or isolate each singer. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-28 | SPV2-OBS-E09-21, SPV2-OBS-E09-22, SPV2-OBS-E09-27, SPV2-OBS-E09-28, SPV2-OBS-E09-29. Fatigue and astonishment initially allow Haru's old interpretation. The four answer in distinct, future-oriented terms before she leaves: chase, beat her in singing, catch up and overtake respectively. Ren's own wish for skill remains an important counterexample to a simple hostile-versus-supportive contrast. SPV2-CH-RIO, SPV2-CH-KYOKA, SPV2-CH-MOMIJI, SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION, SPV2-REL-HARU-REN. | High for statements and pictured recovery, medium for a change that will hold in future contests. They may still have been hurt or surprised. The group's performance is their test on this occasion, not proof that nobody anywhere could be hurt by a disparity. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-29 | SPV2-OBS-E09-27, SPV2-OBS-E09-28, SPV2-OBS-E09-29. Kyoka holds both the returning pair and Haru accountable as peers. Haru responds with an apology, a thank-you and an embrace directed at Kyoka. A changed answer to visible distress can coexist with an unresolved former friendship. SPV2-REL-HARU-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-HARU, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-YUKINE, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-MOMIJI, SPV2-REL-HARU-REN. | High for the immediate change and restored five. Haru is never shown checking her interpretation with Ren, selling out Nakano or undertaking tomorrow's training within E09. Future ordinary conflict would test persistence. The later illustrated ensemble hug does not change who embraced whom backstage. |

### Japanese evidence and claim definitions

Times use the E09 release picture clock. C refers to cue_index in analysis_output/shinepost_ep09_jp-hardsub_screenshots/dialogue_index.json. Raw refers to that directory's subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass or the release's E09 jpsc/jptc files as specified. Dialogue ordinals and physical lines are both one-based. TXT is fan transcription, FRM includes native lettering, INF is interpretive. Heard wording and performed voice identity are open.

| SPV2-CLM-E09-31 | The publicity report supplies a contemporary public image of HY:RAIN against which Haru's private memory can be read. The lyric's “you” could invite a personal association. The clip offers no stated dedication to Haru. | SPV2-OBS-E09-31, SPV2-OBS-E09-33 |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-32 | Haru's account distinguishes two decisions. A completed joint admission and a subsequent internal center evaluation have different stakes. The latter shows Haru discovering how people watch her dance, with the official outcome left unspecified. | SPV2-OBS-E09-32, SPV2-OBS-E09-33 |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-33 | Ren's first-person wish makes Haru an example she wants to emulate. Haru interprets real distress as proof that her own talent harms people. Ren's full motive and her view of Haru leaving remain open. | SPV2-OBS-E09-32, SPV2-OBS-E09-33 |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-34 | The initial agreement bounds Haru's restraint by an assessment of the others' development. That private threshold later places her in the position of choosing for the very people she means to protect. | SPV2-OBS-E09-34, SPV2-OBS-E09-37 |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-35 | The trio's earlier repertoire poses practical problems for five performers. Shared learned choreography makes the four girls' pledge credible. | SPV2-CLM-E09-36 | Naoki recalls a commitment already present in Haru's words. His parallel history and the quotation let him frame his appeal as personal reciprocity. | SPV2-CLM-E09-37 | The strongest contextual referent for the inadequacy Haru remembers perceiving and the protection she wanted to offer is her teammates. | SPV2-CLM-E09-38 | Kyōka's emcee speech supplies the missing object of the title's verb in the request “trust us.” The insert lyric broadens who can offer and receive light, while its simultaneous lower line locates a single dream within plural stars. ### Music and performance evidence definitions

Frozen 2026-11-22, on the E09 horizon. [Source and intake register](../01%20Source%20and%20Intake/SHINEPOST_V2_SOURCE_AND_INTAKE_REGISTER.md). All clocks below are zero-origin programme seconds. FRM f#### identifies the six-digit JPG prefix in SPV2-W-E09-ZIP. JP cue numbers are chronological rows of `dialogue_index.csv`. ASS event numbers count physical Dialogue lines of SPV2-W-E09-JP (`subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass`). FRM times labeled PTS are source presentation timestamps of the manifest’s nominal frame from SPV2-W-E09-SRC. AM is the PCM-verified Japanese stereo main SPV2-W-E09-AO. Paired AM figures give stereo mean-power RMS dBFS and percentage of measured time below −45 dBFS, using whole 10 ms bins. Direct hearing (AO), continuous motion and audiovisual synchronization (CAV) remain OPEN. A visual foreground, written lyric, credited voice actor and perceptible lead voice are separate findings.

| SPV2-CLM-E09-45 | Haru can now find evidence for her partners' agency in performances she previously took as evidence of their need for protection. | SPV2-OBS-E09-46, SPV2-OBS-E09-47. | SPV2-CLM-E09-46 | Kyoka makes the public explanation of the group's name a direct request to its central member, leaving her time to respond before announcing music. | SPV2-OBS-E09-47, SPV2-OBS-E09-48. | SPV2-CLM-E09-47 | The song lets Haru be legible at full effort while retaining a recognisable place for the other four. Programme address distinct from the diegetic encore. Reading depends on image sequence and lyrics, not an identified auditory crossfade. |

### Visual and embodied performance claims

| Claim | Interpretation | Dependencies, alternatives and revision |
|---|---|---|
| SPV2-CLM-E09-51 | Ren first reaches Haru at the scale of public celebrity. Their school friendship restores the physical scale at which they once knew one another. | SPV2-OBS-E09-51 and SPV2-OBS-E09-52. The screen carries an edited promotional performance. A reciprocal street encounter would require a shared physical space. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-52 | The commercial catchphrase and doughnuts give the pair an ordinary shared history before their professional history. | SPV2-OBS-E09-52. The absurd disagreement over taste also works as a self-contained school joke. Haru's narration supplies the longevity of the friendship. |
| SPV2-CLM-E09-53 | The trainees' common life gets its weight from small changes of scale, from a row of exercising bodies to heads close around a tablet. | SPV2-OBS-E09-53. A training montage compresses many undated days. These pictures establish companionship more securely than exact training duration. |

### Open questions at the E10 boundary

- SPV2-OPEN-E10-01 · What does Haru want? Haru tries to interrupt the bargain and says it concerns her. Ren and Kyoka decide its terms. A direct answer to an invitation she is free to accept or refuse would change SPV2-CLM-E10-12 and SPV2-CLM-E10-15. Track SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-CH-KYOKA and SPV2-REL-KYOKA-HARU.
- SPV2-OPEN-E10-02 · Which test will count? The tickets are sold. Yuki's original condition concerns filling a venue. Kyoka has separately offered to show Ren what TINGS can do for Haru. Actual attendance, the work of the five onstage, Yuki's decision, Ren's response and the capacity Kyoka can name for herself each require their own witness. Affects SPV2-CLM-E01-04, SPV2-CLM-E10-03, SPV2-CLM-E10-17, SPV2-CLM-E10-20 and SPV2-CH-KYOKA.
- SPV2-OPEN-E10-03 · The origin of Ren's judgments and her account of the separation. Her present training and purpose are attested. Her detailed four-part critique and statements about seeing their stage do not identify a source. Compare a named attendance, recording, conversation or recollection with SPV2-CLM-E10-11, SPV2-CLM-E10-12, SPV2-CLM-E10-13 and SPV2-OPEN-E09-01. Her colleagues' involvement in the earlier personnel changes also remains open.
- SPV2-OPEN-E10-04 · Who reached whom in the sales campaign? A classmate receives an invitation, a caller discusses a ticket, fan statements circulate and the five appear at FFF. A purchase attributed to an individual needs an actual transaction or testimony. Affects SPV2-CLM-E10-07, SPV2-CLM-E10-09, SPV2-CLM-E10-10 and SPV2-CLM-E10-11. Ren's depicted receipt supplies evidence for this particular exchange.
- SPV2-OPEN-E10-05 · Consequential audiovisual form. Reinspect the recorded dance lesson, OP and campaign return, FFF appearance, Ren/Kyoka admonition, complete Budokan playback and return to the meeting room and the programme ending. Confirm heard repetitions, lines, blend, crowd, transitions and sustained movement only where the source resolves them. Affects SPV2-PF-E10-05, SPV2-PF-E10-07, SPV2-PF-E10-08, SPV2-PF-E10-10, SPV2-CLM-E10-15 and SPV2-CLM-E10-19. Neither source style labels nor stereo power identifies the singers of a line.
- SPV2-OPEN-E10-06 · Music and private communication. Naoki asks about Kei's new song and her ギリギリ leaves completion uncertain. Neither the work's performer nor the song itself is supplied on the call. GYB!! is an identified, recorded public work. A declared dedication, production source or independently documented commissioning relationship would be needed to make the lyrics biographical. Affects SPV2-CLM-E10-07, SPV2-CLM-E10-19 and SPV2-PF-E10-06.

### Longitudinal transitions at E10

- SPV2-REV-E10-01 · STRENGTHEN the written basis of SPV2-CLM-E09-18 and SPV2-OPEN-E09-02 with SPV2-OBS-E10-01. In the E09 instructions for training, C1076 has 明天; the E10 reprise C4 reads 明日. Preserve the original row and its reading-time uncertainty. The embrace and the next training goal recur. E10 does not show how the next rehearsal went. Affects SPV2-CH-HARU and SPV2-REL-KYOKA-HARU.
- SPV2-REV-E10-02 · REVISE the unanswered portion of SPV2-OPEN-E09-01 in light of SPV2-OBS-E10-12 and SPV2-OBS-E10-15. Ren explicitly says she trained to receive Haru's full effort and wants them to shine together again. Preserve Haru's earlier inference in SPV2-CLM-E09-04 as her belief at the time. Ren's understanding of the original decision remains open under SPV2-OPEN-E10-03. Affects SPV2-CH-HARU and the present relationship of the two former teammates.
- SPV2-REV-E10-03 · REVISE SPV2-OPEN-E09-05 and strengthen the distinction made in SPV2-CLM-E09-20. The current four-name card identifies Hiumi Nanoka, 氷海菜花 with the printed reading ひうみなのか. This corrects the romanization “Nana” in the retained E09 professional-roster discussion. Nanoka's first greeting to Haru qualifies any supposition that the two trained together. The recruitment date and basis are still open. SPV2-OBS-E10-14, SPV2-CLM-E10-14, SPV2-PF-E10-10.
- SPV2-REV-E10-04 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E07-04 and SPV2-OBS-E07-04. E07 showed Naoki looking at an order page. In the conversation recorded at SPV2-OBS-E10-07 he tells Kei he already sent the roll tower. The exchange establishes the reported dispatch, and the subsequent argument about paying for a ticket preserves Kei's desire for his attention. Affects SPV2-CH-NAOKI, SPV2-CLM-E10-07 and SPV2-OPEN-E10-04.
- SPV2-REV-E10-05 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E01-04 with SPV2-OBS-E10-03, SPV2-OBS-E10-05 and SPV2-OBS-E10-11. The ratio of the two venues is now stated and the five reach a sellout. Separate the stock of tickets from actual attendance and Yuki's subsequent decision, retained under SPV2-OPEN-E10-02. Affects SPV2-CH-YUKI, SPV2-CH-NAOKI and SPV2-CH-HARU.
- SPV2-REV-E10-06 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E04-06 through SPV2-OBS-E10-10 and SPV2-OBS-E10-17. Kyoka takes an actual poster to her old classmate in the fast-food setting where spontaneous recognition formerly belonged to fantasy. Later the recurrence of Ren's “but that's all” preserves the cost of being seen principally for reliability. This adds a situation in which Kyoka positively seeks a particular person's attention, and leaves the earlier customers' ignorance intact. Affects SPV2-CH-KYOKA and SPV2-CLM-E10-17.

| SPV2-OBS-E10-25 | TXT #981–1013 (song captions followed by thanks and sellout), embedded figures. FRM 000739, 000749, 000754–000775, 000778–000787, 000823, 000834–000847, 000867–000886, 000900. Compare E04 FRM 000384, E05 000404, 000721. | Haru has a stack at school. Homare reaches for it during an interruption by a passerby. By 720.219 both hold flyers. Kyoka holds her flyer up to her schoolfriend 池柴朱 who, interrupted over her burger, smiles and raises a fan of dark anniversary tickets she already has (735.8–736.15). Rio faces a camera with peppers and an orange-clad director. Haru presents a Kantendo pudding to the late-working Naoki in the others' company. The blonde FFF presenter closely matches Nanami's E05 appearance. She gestures toward the five on FFF's tour stage. Remaining stock falls through 135 and 15 with later glimpses of 9 and 4 before the five celebrate the sellout. |
| SPV2-OBS-E10-26 | TXT #1014–1061. FRM 000909, 000921, 000952, 000960–000975, 000986–001007. | Ren describes working to receive Haru's full effort, seeks her return, brushes off questions from present teammates, and calls them backing dancers. In order she targets Rio's dancing while singing, Momiji's dance-only characterization, Yukine's expressive claims, then Kyoka's accuracy without further distinction. Rio points out the detail in an assessment offered as needing no viewing. |
| SPV2-OBS-E10-27 | TXT #1062–1082, 1097–1101. FRM 001010, 001014, 001019–001035, 001077, 001081, 001084. E09 FRM 001335, E10 cast 001316. | Three former colleagues and a fourth visitor appear. Nanoka explicitly gives her name and says this is a first meeting with Haru. Aoba visibly speaks the apology during C1071–1072; Yawara calls Ren home as leader, and Aoba and Nanoka follow up the admonition under C1077–1079. Public cards at 001027 show Aoba, Itoha, Nanoka and Yawara in that order. Aoba and Itoha turn back, and Haru calls both by name. |
| SPV2-OBS-E10-28 | TXT #1081–1116. FRM 001035, 001039–001069, 001073–001077, 001091–001099, 001107–001116. | Kyoka offers Nakano as evidence that TINGS can make Haru shine. Ren adds the condition that Haru come back if they cannot demonstrate it. Kyoka accepts, Haru asks both to calm down and protests being silenced. Ren's green sleeve and Naoki's white sleeve meet over a bag. Rio later identifies Kantendo pudding and revises her first judgment. Haru asks Kyoka why. Yukine commends the challenge. The isolated remark about never making mistakes returns from Kyoka's encounter. |
| SPV2-OBS-E10-29 | TXT #1116–1155. FRM 001120–001134, 001147, 001179–001211, 001264, 001281–001310. | Naoki treats the opponent as difficult and organizes a viewing. Naoki estimates the Budokan crowd at roughly ten thousand. Momiji repeats it and Yukine compares it to five Nakano houses. The sequence expands the represented performance before returning to their faces. Haru describes the members as amazing. Kyoka asks if these are the people they must convince. The recording and this group's reaction precede any judgment at Nakano. |

### Character and relationship claims

| Claim | Interpretation | Confidence, contrary evidence, revision and dependents |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-21 | Having a particular admirer changes the way the returnees speak about professional responsibility. | High for Momiji's surprise and Yukine's reported obligation, moderate for how long either outlook will last. A familiar honorific or a big crowd alone would only show reception. Compare what each does for that fan at a later appearance. SPV2-OBS-E10-21, SPV2-CH-DOOR-COMPANION, SPV2-CH-MOMIJI, SPV2-REL-YUKINEFAN-YUKINE, SPV2-REL-MOMIJIFAN-MOMIJI. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-22 | Rio's distrust of two opposite readings dramatizes how little timely information effort supplies. | High for the two jokes and her anxiety. Her prediction draws on real newcomers and real work. She may simply be playing up surprise the second time. Recover an independent sales history before assigning a marginal conversion to a given performance. SPV2-OBS-E10-21, SPV2-OBS-E10-22, SPV2-OBS-E10-24, SPV2-OBS-E10-25, SPV2-CH-RIO, SPV2-CH-NAOKI. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-23 | Kei seeks inclusion and stimulation and recognizes strain in a friend she wants to hear from. | High for the explicit wish, moderate for relative personal/professional motives. The state of Kei's creative work matters independently. Her reassurance precedes Naoki's proof of fan circulation. A ticket, appearance or explanation of her information would settle different questions. SPV2-OBS-E10-23, SPV2-OBS-E10-24, SPV2-REL-KEI-NAOKI, SPV2-REL-NAOKI-KEI. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-24 | Fans exercise independent judgment about different members before helping to sell one event. | High for pictured endorsements and Naoki's attribution. Individual sales and the purple poster's personal name are unresolved. Check stronger identification and any account of how a buyer actually discovered TINGS. SPV2-OBS-E10-21, SPV2-OBS-E10-24, SPV2-OBS-E10-25, SPV2-CH-SATO, SPV2-CH-TOKKA, SPV2-CH-FUMU, SPV2-REL-FANS-TRIO. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-25 | Classmates and manager are also people to whom the idols can offer something on ordinary terms. | High for Homare, Kyoka's earlier schoolfriend and Haru's pudding gesture. The schoolfriend already has tickets to show Kyoka. The montage supplies neither a spoken promise to attend nor a record of the earlier purchase. At the office Naoki turns toward the five and the pudding they bring him. The pepper image revisits a job without establishing Rio's private taste changed. SPV2-OBS-E10-25, SPV2-CH-HOMARE, SPV2-CH-KYOKA-SCHOOLFRIEND, SPV2-REL-HARU-NAOKI, SPV2-CLM-E04-06, SPV2-OBS-E05-07. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-26 | Ren's renewed approach revises the idea that an injured friend's sole wish was for Haru to hold back. | High for Ren's stated purpose, moderate for her interpretation of what is best for Haru. Competitive ambition, attachment and protection can coexist. Her four dismissals, the specificity of her knowledge and pudding complicate a single verdict. Test against Haru's own answer and Ren's source of information. SPV2-OBS-E10-26, SPV2-OBS-E10-28, SPV2-REL-REN-HARU, SPV2-REL-REN-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-REN-RIO, SPV2-REL-REN-YUKINE, SPV2-REL-REN-MOMIJI. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-27 | HY:RAIN's current five have different kinds of authority and acquaintance at this visit. | High for the cards, Nanoka's introduction, old colleagues' recognition and attempt to retrieve the leader. An apology does not prove disagreement with all Ren's views. A separate conversation would test whether any colleague authorized recruitment or knew the terms beforehand. SPV2-OBS-E10-27, SPV2-OBS-E10-28, SPV2-CH-NANOKA, SPV2-CH-YAWARA, SPV2-CH-ITOHA, SPV2-CH-AOBA, SPV2-REL-NANOKA-HARU. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-28 | Kyoka's defense protects the group while accepting a condition Haru never authorizes. | High for acceptance, Haru's protest and later question. The recalled criticism supports personal hurt without proving it caused the wager. Supporting TINGS' competence in chorus does not document three more assents to the price Ren adds later. Ask separately who agrees to which proposition. SPV2-OBS-E10-26, SPV2-OBS-E10-27, SPV2-OBS-E10-28, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-HARU, SPV2-REL-KYOKA-REN, SPV2-REL-HARU-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-YUKINE-KYOKA. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-29 | The recording makes the target tangible and exposes different stakes for Haru and Kyoka. | High for the contrasting verbal responses and venue comparison. Scale may account for part of the reaction. The sung text about recovery resonates with the visit without supplying a factual message from Ren to Haru or an adjudication. Actual Nakano reception or an individual account could revise the forecast. SPV2-OBS-E10-27, SPV2-OBS-E10-28, SPV2-OBS-E10-29, SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-CH-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-HARU-REN, SPV2-PF-E10-10. |

### Japanese observation and claim definitions

C numbers refer to cue_index in analysis_output/shinepost_ep10_jp-hardsub_screenshots/dialogue_index.json. Raw event references give one-based Dialogue ordinal and physical line in that bundle's subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass (selected) and the original release E10 .jpsc.ass / .jptc.ass (original). Times are elapsed original picture. TXT is transcript evidence, FRM is visible evidence, INF is interpretation. Audio delivery and performer-by-syllable assignments remain open.

### Independent music and performance definitions

Frozen 2026-11-22. [Source and intake register](../01%20Source%20and%20Intake/SHINEPOST_V2_SOURCE_AND_INTAKE_REGISTER.md). All times in programme seconds. FRM f#### is the numeric prefix of the individual picture in SPV2-W-E10-ZIP. Times printed as PTS come from the corresponding nominal-frame ordinal in the original SPV2-W-E10-SRC. JP cue is a chronological row in `dialogue_index.csv`; raw ASS is physical Dialogue order in SPV2-W-E10-JP, `subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass`. AM denotes measurements of the verified Japanese main-stereo SPV2-W-E10-AO, bound to that edition. For paired AM results, first value is programme RMS in dBFS, second is the percentage of eligible mixed sound below −45 dBFS. Nonmusical performances have descriptive classifications. AO listening and CAV continuous movement/synchronization remain OPEN.

### Claims

| ID | Claim | Grounds, alternative or test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-41 | The ticket table gives specific purchasers a way to answer what the five-member performance made them notice. Local enthusiasm alone cannot fill a venue twenty times larger. | SPV2-OBS-E10-41, SPV2-OBS-E10-44. Two personal testimonies and the manager's explicit size comparison support different scales of inference. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-42 | The retrospective stitches earlier performers and audiences into the story of three months’ progress. Their achieved club and mall encounters explain the members' initial confidence. | SPV2-OBS-E10-42, SPV2-OBS-E10-41. Prior successes can also make an early sales ceiling harder to imagine. Audio reuse under each image is open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-43 | The campaign distributes professional tasks among the five. A recording can translate Momiji's balancing skill into a repeatable invitation for remote spectators. | SPV2-OBS-E10-44, SPV2-OBS-E10-45. The invitation is spoken and the production device shown. No viewer is observed executing the routine or buying a ticket because of it. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-44 | The second Wonder Starter changes the audience of an established song by giving the baton lyric to images of ordinary people passing the group on to others. | SPV2-OBS-E10-43, SPV2-OBS-E10-47. Words, waveform and varied locations support an editorial musical sequence. Exact alternate master and per-line ownership remain open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-45 | The visible spreading of recommendations makes the eventual sellout an accomplishment with participants beyond performers and management. | SPV2-OBS-E10-46, SPV2-OBS-E10-47. Reported video sharing, personal endorsements and counter sequence support the contribution; quantities per channel and day-of-show attendance remain open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E10-46 | FFF lends the quintet access to a room of spectators who are present for another act. | Claim | Formulation and scope | Evidence and countercheck |

| SPV2-CLM-E11-01 | Kei knows enough to tease and to seek a seat. The sellout has consequences before the concert takes place. Her contact with Haru and the completion of any text remain unspecified. | SPV2-OBS-E11-01. Check Yuki's own decision against the telephone inference. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-02 | Five bodies register the work with different postures. One visit gives no stable ranking of endurance. The title creates an expectation whose outcome the cutoff keeps pending. | SPV2-OBS-E11-02. A continuous inspection could establish more of the practice transition. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-03 | The disagreement qualifies any simple rule that HY:RAIN only values absolute precision. | SPV2-CLM-E11-04 | The public single pursues Kyoka into an everyday space. Announced latest single and audible source correspondence are independently identifiable. | SPV2-CLM-E11-05 | Ren's pause has become evidence to Kyoka that she makes no impression. | SPV2-CLM-E11-08 | The remembered trio's companionship combines play, opinion and helping with practical things. The performer on the earlier home screen cannot be named solely by analogy with the later, named Budokan set. | SPV2-OBS-E11-08. Ren arranges the evidence for her present argument. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-09 | Homare's skepticism about a commercial coexists with active participation in fandom. Her later refusal is specific to becoming a performer. | SPV2-CLM-E11-10 | Ren infers a purposive skill from looking both at the stage and at the people it reaches. The recurring show supports several different reasons for wanting this work. | SPV2-CLM-E11-11 | They undertook a course of action together. An indefinite obligation of exclusivity is Ren's interpretation. Homare’s chosen way of accompanying them supplies a real alternative to it. | SPV2-OBS-E11-11. The legible notice is addressed to Haru alone. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-12 | The two accounts specify two experiences of the same rupture. Haru's interpretation of distress and Ren's later self-reproach can both be true. | SPV2-CLM-E11-13 | Rebuilding marks a concrete professional answer to the loss. Ren attributes a purpose to the collective labour. An acting shoot is visually differentiated from an ordinary domestic scene. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-14 | The criticism is partly firsthand. Ren distinguishes the modesty of the stage from the fact that Haru is with somebody else. Do not carry either song over a picture quotation without a sound-specific route. |

### Open questions at the E11 boundary

- SPV2-OPEN-E11-01 · Naoki's proposal. What exactly is the one method in SPV2-OBS-E11-20? Does Kyoka choose it, how does she perform it and does anyone respond as she hoped? Until a stated instruction or visible act, preserve its status as an offer. Affects SPV2-CH-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-NAOKI-KYOKA and SPV2-PF-E11-15.
- SPV2-OPEN-E11-02 · Three audiences for the anniversary. Distinguish an occupied house and Yuki's formal action, Ren's personal response, and the effect of Kyoka's particular performance on other attendees. Kei's inability to buy a ticket and Naoki's reference to 2,000 do not count audience entrances. SPV2-OBS-E11-01, SPV2-OBS-E11-15, SPV2-OBS-E11-20; SPV2-OPEN-E10-02.
- SPV2-OPEN-E11-03 · What particular occasions underlie each of Ren's judgments? Her presence at the E04 Bright Station concert and E06 mall is visible. Evidence for her observation of the later full five, and her knowledge of how they rehearsed, remains incomplete. An identified attendance, recording or testimony could distinguish the routes. SPV2-OBS-E11-14, SPV2-CLM-E11-14, SPV2-REL-REN-KYOKA.
- SPV2-OPEN-E11-04 · What does Kei expect Naoki to recognize? The credits and the caller's vocabulary allow more than one explanation. Open also are the completion and intended use of the outstanding lyrics. An identified encounter, explicit disclosure or work credit would discriminate. SPV2-CH-KEI, SPV2-CH-HOTARU, SPV2-REL-KEI-NAOKI, SPV2-OBS-E11-01, SPV2-OBS-E11-19.
- SPV2-OPEN-E11-05 · Reinspect the programme audio and continuous picture for the TV sign-off and near-error discussion, restaurant speaker transitions, childhood television, complete represented Hotaru number and reaction sequence, selection and training cues, filmed GYB!! screen reveal, TINGS excerpts and spoken interruptions at the park and meeting. Which words and musical phrases are heard, in what balance and by whom? Match mix layers and all visible/audible boundaries before assigning individual sung parts, prosody, synchrony, or offscreen speakers. SPV2-OBS-E11-03, SPV2-OBS-E11-04, SPV2-OBS-E11-10, SPV2-OBS-E11-13, SPV2-OBS-E11-17, SPV2-OBS-E11-18; SPV2-PF-E11-08, SPV2-PF-E11-12.
- SPV2-OPEN-E11-06 · What does the report to TINGS include? Haru names Kyoka as the person who told. Kyoka heard Ren's account directly and speaks about it. The act of telling and its motive are elided, and Ren's colleagues' search does not establish that they heard the park exchange. An identified later retelling could change the scope of SPV2-CLM-E11-16 and SPV2-CLM-E11-18 and the directed Haru, Kyoka and Ren records.

### Longitudinal transitions at E11

- SPV2-REV-E11-01 · REVISE SPV2-OPEN-E10-01. Yesterday Haru's intervention left the terms unsettled; today she declines Ren's invitation, asks her to watch and insists on the name of her present group. SPV2-OBS-E11-15 and SPV2-CLM-E11-15 establish her choice before the concert. Retain the E10 observations as dated. Record the answer under SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-REL-HARU-REN and SPV2-REL-REN-HARU; her answer does not settle SPV2-OPEN-E11-02.
- SPV2-REV-E11-02 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E09-04 and REVISE the remaining uncertainty in SPV2-OPEN-E09-01 and SPV2-REV-E10-02. Ren remembers the first promise, her own sense of failure, the empty locker and the training that followed. SPV2-OBS-E11-11, SPV2-OBS-E11-12, SPV2-OBS-E11-13 distinguish what Haru saw from what Ren later said. Preserve SPV2-CH-REN, SPV2-CH-HARU, SPV2-REL-REN-HARU, SPV2-PF-E11-10 and SPV2-PF-E11-11 with both viewpoints.
- SPV2-REV-E11-03 · REVISE SPV2-OPEN-E10-03 with SPV2-OBS-E11-14. Ren sees the original trio on the Brightest profile, observes the E04 theatre closer and is present for the E06 mall embrace. What she knows of Kyoka's earlier failure, Rio's work beforehand, or the later five remains bounded. SPV2-REL-REN-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-REN-RIO, SPV2-CH-REN and SPV2-PF-E11-13.
- SPV2-REV-E11-04 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E09-02 and narrow SPV2-OPEN-E09-05. Homare declines the audition and offers assistance; the first successful applicants are Ren and Haru. Yawara, Itoha and Aoba appear in the group and at the empty locker. Nanoka is introduced afterwards as the newcomer. SPV2-OBS-E11-11, SPV2-OBS-E11-12, SPV2-OBS-E11-13, SPV2-CH-HOMARE, SPV2-REL-REN-HOMARE and SPV2-REL-REN-NANOKA. No precise recruitment date is implied.
- SPV2-REV-E11-05 · STRENGTHEN the earlier spectator history in SPV2-OBS-E04-02, SPV2-OBS-E06-03 and SPV2-OBS-E08-08 through SPV2-OBS-E11-10. The new witness gives Ren's account, seats her with Haru and Homare, and shows Momiji in original footage between two retained pictures. Preserve the individual motives and the limits of mutual knowledge. SPV2-CH-HOTARU, SPV2-CH-REN, SPV2-CH-MOMIJI, SPV2-REL-MOMIJI-HOTARU and SPV2-PF-E11-08.
- SPV2-REV-E11-06 · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E04-06 and SPV2-REV-E10-06. Kyoka remembers Ren's pause as proof she was unmemorable; she subsequently names three specific artistic strengths while discounting the work she can do. The practical rescue and Tokka's earlier response qualify “nothing.” SPV2-OBS-E11-05, SPV2-OBS-E11-07, SPV2-OBS-E11-18, SPV2-CH-KYOKA, SPV2-REL-TOKKA-KYOKA and SPV2-REL-NAOKI-KYOKA. Retain the open proposal in SPV2-OPEN-E11-01.
- SPV2-REV-E11-07 · PRESERVE SPV2-OPEN-E10-06 with narrower object information. At E10 the object of Naoki's question was a new song; at E11 he specifies lyrics. Kei's deflection supplies no completed work or recipient. SPV2-OBS-E11-01, SPV2-OBS-E11-19, SPV2-CH-KEI and SPV2-REL-NAOKI-KEI. Keep shared performer credits distinct from a character disclosure.

### Observations of return, refusal and present capacities

| Observation | Episode source route | Bounded observation |

| SPV2-OBS-E11-21 | TXT #1–22,803–825,831–835; FRM 000003,000019,000030,000256–000290,000303,000318,000337–000355. | Kei could not buy a ticket, wants a seat, knows of the possible recruitment and asks whether Naoki has noticed something about Haru. He asks if she has met her. Lyrics are still an issue for Kei. At HY:RAIN a wrap and dispute about Itoha's near-mistake precede Ren asking to move a check to tomorrow. A doughnut bag helps fellow members guess her purpose. A shop broadcast introduces GYB!!; Kyoka hears Ren's judgment again in recollection. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-22 | TXT #836–887; FRM 000355–000397,000408–000424,000430–000459,000464,000475. | At their old meeting place Haru asks how long it has been since a conversation like this. Ren estimates a year. Haru came after lessons in response to Ren's message. Ren apologizes for arriving unannounced yesterday, acknowledges another purpose and repeats the invitation. Onlookers recognize Ren despite her hood. Kyoka beckons both away, then challenges Ren about the exposure and her renewed approach. Ren corrects the assumption that the promise meant the wager at Nakano. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-23 | TXT #888–925,937–960; FRM 000487–000543,000568–000616,000716,000723–000761; E11 billing 001321. | Ren remembers Haru running with her and Homare giving measured advice. The three share food, worksheets, viewing and baking. The classroom doughnut discussion distinguishes Homare's skepticism about an ad from her stated love of Hotaru. Ren invites both friends to audition. Homare declines becoming an idol, teases Ren about going alone and offers support. Haru voices uncertainty. The two clasp hands, then pass the first audition they try. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-24 | TXT #926–936,939–945; FRM 000625–000647,000658–000672,000679–000706,000716–000730. | Haru, Ren and Homare wear matching concert shirts. The Budokan views include Kyoka, Rio with Aoi, Yukine and a short original image of Momiji with a pink light at 598.35–599.51, apart from the three friends. Ren revises her description of the crowd being absorbed into one of Hotaru actively drawing them in. She names voice, radiance, heat and gaze. She subsequently cites this experience as her reason for wanting to move a crowd. No depicted greeting from stage to one named individual. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-25 | TXT #961–977; FRM 000782–000815,000822–000849,000853–000880; E09 OBS 21–22. | Ren names Yawara, Itoha, Aoba and praises the former lineup's skill, commitment and shared prospects. At the selection she recognizes a gulf. FRM 000849 places Haru outside Ren's crying scene. Later a locker carries Haru's name and is empty. Ren apologizes there for failing to keep up and keep the promise. Yawara, Itoha and Aoba stand with her. This later apology has no visible Haru recipient. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-26 | TXT #977–1001, with separate music events #989,991,994; FRM 000883–000911,000916–000955,000961–000993. | Ren reports renewed basic vocal work, doubled dance lessons, revised meals and acting assignments. Images pair her with Aoba, Itoha and Yawara across different tasks. Nanoka newly joins. A publicity/video passage leads to Ren's estimate that she can now keep up. She learns of Haru's group; Brightest profile on phone. Hooded Ren is pictured in context with a live by the trio and the mall embrace. She objects both to the stage's level and to Haru performing with other people. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-27 | TXT #1002–1032; FRM 001003–001075,001079–001110. | Haru apologizes, refuses to rejoin, describes herself as TINGS' Haru and invites Ren to watch Nakano. Ren is silent for an interval and says she will not give up. Messages from Yawara and Nanoka involve the outing. The four colleagues search a place Ren has told them about. A doughnut bag goes to Haru. Ren requests secrecy, is spotted and denies fleeing as she leaves. Kyoka and Haru remain. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-28 | TXT #1032–1077; FRM 001108–001149,001155–001204,001208–001226. | Kyoka expresses admiration for Ren's rebuilding. Haru anticipates a worry that she might do better elsewhere. Names Yuki's recruitment, Kyoka, Rio, Yukine, Momiji and Naoki as people who restored her belief that she could perform fully. Connects her Shine Post aim to TINGS. She asks Kyoka to keep this meeting from the others to spare them worry. In the meeting room the others discuss the old promise with doughnuts in hand. Haru blames Kyoka for telling. The act of disclosure is elided. |
| SPV2-OBS-E11-29 | TXT #1076–1119; FRM 001226–001251,001257–001296,001307–001313. | The others empathize with Ren's commitment without giving up Haru. Kyoka names Rio's moving voice, Momiji's startling dance and Yukine's expressive appeal. Claims she has nothing comparable, is merely accurate, and doubts whether she personally could satisfy Ren. Momiji recalls the stubborn paper metaphor. Haru appeals to Naoki. He doubts accuracy alone will satisfy two thousand customers and offers one method specifically available to Kyoka. The method and her answer remain undisclosed. |

### Character and relationship claims

| Claim | Interpretation | Confidence, competing evidence, revision and dependents |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-21 | A sold-out event and an apparently successful take each leave somebody asking what still requires work. | High for the distinct tasks in speech. Kei's prior knowledge is evidence of access, not an identification as Hotaru or eyewitness to Ren's visit. Aoba's correction need not mean Itoha missed the movement. An explicit meeting or completed broadcast check would constrain those readings. OBS 21; CH-KEI/NAOKI/AOBA/ITOHA. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-22 | Kyoka can protect a rival in public while personally hurt by that rival's judgment. | High for her escape route and reprimand; moderate for protective motive toward Ren, since Haru is equally exposed. Ren apologizes for how she visited and immediately renews her request. Her commitment and public imprudence can coexist. Test against how she responds to a refusal. OBS 21–22,27; REL-KYOKA-REN, REL-REN-HARU. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-23 | Homare's choice to stay an audience member was a positive commitment within the original friendship. | High for her refusal and promised help; later E02/E03/E10 conduct is congruent. She may also have independent reasons for declining which the scene leaves unspoken. There is no promise all three will debut. OBS 23–24; CH-HOMARE, REL-HOMARE-REN/HARU, E10 flyer record. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-24 | Ren remembers Hotaru as someone who actively makes an audience feel something and wants to do that herself. | High for first-person change of wording and later invitation. Friendship and fear of undertaking an audition alone also inform the proposal. Audience cuts establish co-presence without a conversation among later TINGS members. Test separately against other spectators' memories. OBS 23–24; CH-HOTARU/REN, REL-REN-HOTARU. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-25 | Two friends experience the same lost partnership as their own failure, with different evidence available to each. | High for Ren's own apology and Haru's E09 account. An empty locker and the groupmates' arrival deny Haru access to this apology at the time. Ren's belief anyone but Haru had reached a limit is an evaluation. A dated direct exchange would change their mutual-knowledge boundary. OBS 25–27; CH-REN/HARU, both directed edges. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-26 | Ren's wish to recover the partnership combines painstaking professional preparation with distress at being replaced in Haru's life. | High for the two causes she states. Only moderate for how every other HY:RAIN member understood the training; the shared routine establishes participation. Her eyewitness views test E10's open question about exposure, not whether every judgment is fair. OBS 25–27; CH-NANOKA, original members, E10 CLM 26–27. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-27 | Haru now owns a decision the E10 wager had assigned to two other people. | High for no return and a direct invitation to witness the group's full work. The refusal is independent of a future result. Ren's determination to persist is equally direct; no acceptance, consent to enforcement or reconciliation follows from the doughnuts. Test later against each participant's decisions. OBS 26–28; REL-HARU-REN, REL-REN-HARU, REL-HARU-KYOKA. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-28 | Haru's positive reason for staying concerns permission to perform and a shared ambition; relief about her answer need not cure Kyoka's doubt. | High for naming the people and saying she believes in this future; moderate for how accurately she has guessed what Kyoka was thinking. Group discussion establishes that some news traveled, while the circumstances and precise extent of disclosure remain open. OBS 27–29; CH-HARU/KYOKA/YUKI/NAOKI, prior E01/E09 aims. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-29 | Kyoka's claim that she contributes nothing turns an external insult into an individual test the group cannot answer with competitive slogans. | High for her redistribution of evidence and Naoki's audience-based counterquestion. His agreement concerns her present approach. E03–06 provide contrary examples of reaching people, including Tokka. No evidence yet settles what he proposes, whether only Kyoka can carry it out or whether she will try. OBS 21–22,28–29; CH-KYOKA, REL-NAOKI-KYOKA, E04/E06 and next-stage evidence. |

### Japanese evidence definitions

Release-relative sources throughout: C = cue_index in E11 dialogue_index.json; S = one-based Dialogue ordinal in subtitles/selected_subtitles.ass; O = one-based Dialogue ordinal in the release’s E11 .jpsc.ass and .jptc.ass. Native times are in the corresponding E11 .mkv. TXT is fan text, FRM is visible picture/print, and INF is bounded interpretation. No voice heard directly.

Later five-member attendance, lyric dedication, authorship and the full history of her viewing remain open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-37 | Haru answers for herself and offers Ren a spectator’s place at Nakano; Ren preserves both affection and disagreement. The chat shows colleagues piecing together a likely location. | OBS-E11-37; C1002–1019, 15:26.61–16:35.53. Haru’s declaration supplies her present choice. It does not predict a concert outcome or establish that everyone heard the explanatory flashback. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-38 | Haru voices a hypothesis about Kyōka’s thought and grounds her choice in others’ restored permission to try. The group learns the private history offscreen. Deference and worry can coexist with disclosure. | OBS-E11-38; C1035–1057, C1062–1073, 17:06.37–19:11.14. Both editions preserve the question; neither resolves はう？ or 叶えなれる by ear. Kyōka’s motive for telling is open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-39 | The question moves from collective doubt to Kyōka’s own fear. Naoki expands the stakes to an audience and reverses her “nothing” with an exclusive “only you.” The E11 ending supplies a way to imagine individuality among five, while the concert solution stays undisclosed. | OBS-E11-39; C1081, C1090–1102, C1106–1119 and C1123–1127. C1099/C1109 contain suspect Japanese; context and both translations support error-free execution. No method, assent, outcome or confirmed audience reaction is supplied. |

### Musical and performance observations and claims

Frozen 2026-11-22. JP numbers are chronological cue/raw ASS Dialogue. FRM numbers are retained JPG prefixes and the supplied FRM seconds are packet-derived source PTS. AM is the PCM-verified Japanese main programme. [Source and intake register](../01%20Source%20and%20Intake/SHINEPOST_V2_SOURCE_AND_INTAKE_REGISTER.md).

#### Claims

| Stable ID | Definition | Support and counter-reading |
|---|---|---|
| SPV2-CLM-E11-41 | The credited five-person themes frame Kyoka as a group member before a specific new contribution is named. | SPV2-OBS-E11-41, SPV2-OBS-E11-73, SPV2-OBS-E11-74. Repeated series billing can also supply a familiar frame independently of her predicament. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-42 | The disagreement about Itoha’s recovery establishes internal professional disagreement about what counts as success before Kyoka accepts the rival's verdict. | SPV2-OBS-E11-42, SPV2-OBS-E11-73. This exchange cannot grade the unshown move or certify that Aoba speaks for the whole group. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-43 | The in-store single gives Ren public reach even while her earlier hesitation becomes personally painful for Kyoka. | SPV2-OBS-E11-43. Repeated waveform matches, location and words support the juxtaposition. Listeners in the restaurant need not have any access to Kyoka's memory. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-44 | Imitation and shared fandom allow the three friends to participate before the same concert prompts different commitments from them. | SPV2-OBS-E11-44, SPV2-OBS-E11-45, SPV2-OBS-E11-46. Remaining an informed supporter is Homare’s expressed preference. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-45 | Watching the people around her, as well as Hotaru, gives Ren a specific model of an idol’s ability to affect an audience. | SPV2-OBS-E11-45, SPV2-OBS-E11-46. Ren directly revises her description. The edit can show spectators whom Ren did not personally identify. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-46 | The old HY:RAIN five first look to Ren like a promising, cooperative working group. Her later sense of individual inferiority changes the meaning she gives that work. | SPV2-OBS-E11-47, SPV2-OBS-E11-48. The five's hours together and her praise are attested, technical equality is unmeasured. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-47 | Ren’s empty-locker apology supplies the second side of the door episode. The same distress that prompted Haru to withdraw becomes Ren’s reason to work toward their promise. | SPV2-OBS-E11-48, SPV2-OBS-E11-49. The later words cannot simply be assigned to the earlier encounter. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-48 | HY:RAIN’s renewed capacity is presented as a collective achievement before Ren advances it as a personal claim upon Haru. | SPV2-OBS-E11-49, SPV2-OBS-E11-50. Acting, diet, vocal and dance work distinguish observable practice, stated intent and unmeasured improvement. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-49 | Showing HY:RAIN their own public image and then showing Ren in two TINGS audiences gives her a spectator's experience of both acts. Her admission about Haru limits her standing as an impartial critic. | SPV2-OBS-E11-50, SPV2-OBS-E11-71. Different venue, scale and performance standard remain plausible grounds for a comparative judgment. The final motive is expressly personal. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-50 | Haru's Be Your Light!! visual quotation resolves into a backstage embrace. Her account of being able to give her all includes what her colleagues did after sharing the stage. | SPV2-OBS-E11-72. Simple recollection can also explain the repeated pictures. The park invitation promises a future demonstration only. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-71 | Kyoka fears she cannot cause a comparable response in an audience. Naoki changes the question from satisfying Ren to reaching the ticket holders without yet naming the proposed method. | SPV2-OBS-E11-42, SPV2-OBS-E11-45, SPV2-OBS-E11-73. E04 already showed individual recognition, so Kyoka’s current fear is not a retrospective verdict on all her performances. |

### Visual and embodied-performance claims

| Claim | Observation dependencies | Reading, alternative and revision condition |
|---|---|---|
| SPV2-CLM-E11-51 | SPV2-OBS-E11-51, SPV2-OBS-E11-70 | The unequal recovery postures let the manager see an unfinished task after the sellout. A post-rehearsal pose cannot diagnose fitness or an exact difference in ability. The unseen caller is consistent with the credited Kei, while direct vocal recognition remains open. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-52 | SPV2-OBS-E11-52, SPV2-OBS-E11-64 | Aoba's desire to review a near mistake gives Ren's exacting standards a group context. The unseen television routine offers no basis for judging the step. The bag, and the remarks that notice it, establish the colleagues' later inference about where Ren went. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-53 | SPV2-OBS-E11-53, SPV2-OBS-E11-67 | The criticism has entered Kyoka's ordinary day. Reflection and recalled pointing make its impact visible long before she voices it to her group. The restaurant reflection is a physical double in glass, with no new speaker opposite her. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-54 | SPV2-OBS-E11-54, SPV2-OBS-E11-55 | Kyoka takes a conspicuous initiative precisely because she sees what the more famous visitor has missed. The others follow her escape route. The incident can challenge Ren's personal impression without supplying evidence that a concert audience will notice Kyoka. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-55 | SPV2-OBS-E11-55, SPV2-OBS-E11-56 | Shared food, work and games make a joint future plausible for three children before a career becomes a specific aim. Their visual affinity supports friendship. It supplies no three-person contractual or career promise. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-56 | SPV2-OBS-E11-55, SPV2-OBS-E11-56, SPV2-OBS-E11-64 | The doughnut gift calls up commercial imagery the friends learned to use in their private lives. The graphic establishes continuity of brand and association. It cannot establish that the gift is a bargain, apology or inducement. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-57 | SPV2-OBS-E11-56, SPV2-OBS-E11-57 | Ren's account of becoming an idol among fellow viewers gains a larger history through audience cutaways. E11 places the future TINGS women in this audience. E06 F000450 324.616 and E06 F000457 336.000 support Aoi beside Rio. Blonde waves and a pink braid with a rabbit pin suggest Aoba and Itoha beside Momiji, but those partial faces lack confirmation. A look from Hotaru to Ren would require matched geography. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-58 | SPV2-OBS-E11-55, SPV2-OBS-E11-58 | Homare's affirmative role consists of choosing to support the pair. The wide and the two clasped hands resist the tempting three-person oath. Haru's notice independently verifies her own success. Ren's success is given by the narrative and subsequent training. |
| SPV2-CLM-E11-59 | SPV2-OBS-E11-59, SPV2-OBS-E11-60 | The dancers initially share a work routine and a model to study. A tablet video of five other figures is weak evidence for the viewers' own technical standard. The cut to Ren turning away identifies her experience of the gap without numerically measuring it. |

### E12 audience row and claim scope

**2026-11-23 · REVISE the seat attribution attached to SPV2-OBS-E12-05 and SPV2-OBS-E12-22 in the original chapter. PRESERVE SPV2-CLM-E12-05.** In the front row Nanami voices her worries about Yukine and Momiji. The three turn back, and the native reverse at 266.5 s locates the reassurance in the row behind FFF, where Kikuchi and the Yura Yura Sisters sit. The earlier chapter placed the replying woman in Nanami’s own row. Individual voice identification still requires a Japanese main-stream hearing. Professional familiarity and childhood observation remain the independently supported grounds for the two spectators’ anticipation. The disposition applies to the named component at its episode horizon. OPEN retains the residual in the linked case after the local hearing. STRENGTHEN adds support to the specified proposition.

**E01-club** · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E01-02. The room’s affection and Haru’s private doubt coexist. No audience knowledge of that doubt is shown.

**E01-phone** · OPEN SPV2-OPEN-E01-02. Naoki and Kei can invoke his private idiom through their existing familiarity.

**E01-rehearsal** · PRESERVE SPV2-CLM-E01-13, OPEN SPV2-OPEN-E01-04. Retain Rio’s observable delay and the reproduced music. Individual competence cannot be ranked from this passage.

**E01-motive** · PRESERVE SPV2-CLM-E01-03, STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E01-10, STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E01-15. Haru can deliberately rally her cheerfulness and mean what she says. Rio and Kyoka can have different reasons for withholding their wishes.

**E02-confidence** · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E02-22, OPEN SPV2-OPEN-E02-03. Read the change as a commitment she acts on. Retain the limits of Naoki’s visual cue.

**E02-master** · STRENGTHEN SPV2-CLM-E02-46, OPEN SPV2-OPEN-E02-01. Strengthen the source relationship between the E01 rehearsal and E02 concert. Keep the settings and theatrical presentations distinct.

**E03-pickup** · OPEN SPV2-OPEN-E03-01. Identify the interruption as a spoken あれ?; retain the distinction between dream and historical witness.

**E03-belief** · OPEN SPV2-OPEN-E03-02, PRESERVE SPV2-CLM-E03-35. Preserve duties, loyalties and boundaries as separate assertions. Do not infer consent from the earlier wish.

### E11 | Comparison of voiced forms and the recurring effect

The calibrated recurring chime was undetected in the lossless episode sweep. Negative images and direct audition of the selected controls limit confusion with pale dance sparkles and a celesta-like sound. A non-detection carries the sensitivity of its search and leaves the broader epistemic reading intact. The source reinspection supports the voiced 人に/みんなに divergence, two separate 叶えられる forms and the shared 覚えたことは wording. Yukine’s question carries よ. A complete lossless hearing confirms that Rio’s following どうする has no added よ. See SPV2-CLM-E11-20, SPV2-OBS-E11-38, SPV2-OBS-E11-39 and SPV2-OBS-E11-69.

### E12 | Measured signal, caption divergences and open names

The lossless signal and native pictures converge on the recurring effect in Yuki’s sanction speech. A visual E01 reprise is distinguishable from the newly placed event. Use the cue as evidence of presentation without inferring a particular hidden plan. Keep the reported spoken わけ, を and が paired with their separate written witnesses. Retain the voiced 間違いない correction with the independent lossless pilot and ordinary listening receipt. Later group speech and a precisely timed breath-like catch are admissible at the scope of their source. The rear-row name and exact group endings remain unresolved. See SPV2-OBS-E12-32, SPV2-OBS-E12-33, SPV2-OBS-E12-34, SPV2-OBS-E12-37, SPV2-OBS-E12-62 and SPV2-OBS-E12-69.

The E12 booklet and native hearing support 不揃いな against the selected episode subtitle’s 不揃いの. Commercial part labels corroborate the verse and ensemble-chorus assignments separately from what individual voices can be distinguished in the episode. [Published E10 parts](https://www.joysound.com/web/search/song/957587) · [Published E12 parts](https://www.joysound.com/web/search/song/957586).
